  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子磁矩至今未被精确测定；若其值较大，可能显著影响其在超新星强磁场中的传播行为，进而改变可探测的中微子信号。

Method: 结合简化的解析模型与两类详细数值模拟（17倍太阳质量的中子星前身和26倍太阳质量的黑洞前身磁旋转超新星），计算含非零中微子磁矩时的中微子通量及JUNO/Hyper-K探测器事件率。

Result: 发现10^{-14} μ_B量级的跃迁磁矩即可显著影响中微子驱动超新星的中子化爆发与吸积阶段信号；而对磁旋转超新星，早期内甚至10^{-15} μ_B量级即有可观测效应。

Conclusion: 中微子磁矩即使极小（低至10^{-15} μ_B），亦可在特定超新星场景中产生可观测的中微子信号畸变，为当前及下一代探测器提供关键检验窗口。

Abstract: Despite several decades of searches, the neutrino magnetic moment remains unknown. If it is large, the neutrino evolution can be affected by its coupling to strong magnetic fields like the ones present in at least some core-collapse supernovae. We explore these effects for Majorana neutrinos. We first discuss a schematic model often employed and show the impact of resonant spin-flavor precession on the neutrino signal for a future galactic supernova. Then, we consider detailed simulations of a neutrino-driven supernova with 17 ${\rm M}_{\odot}$ leaving a neutron star and a magnetorotational supernova with 26 ${\rm M}_{\odot}$ with a black-hole remnant. We present predictions for the supernova neutrino fluxes and the event rates in presence of a non-zero neutrino magnetic moment, for the running JUNO and upcoming Hyper-Kamiokande experiments. Our results show a significant impact for transition magnetic moments of $10^{-14} ~μ_B$ during the neutronization burst and the accretion phase for the neutrino driven supernova, and as small as $10^{-15} ~μ_B$ at the early times for the magnetorotational supernova.

</details>


### [98] [Gravitational Waves and Nambu-Goldstone Bosons from Infrared-Finite Cosmic Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08254)
*Hajime Fukuda,Shuailiang Ge,Keisuke Harigaya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在具有自发破缺全局对称性的理论中出现的红外有限宇宙弦（如斯格明子线和半局域弦），指出它们不支持导致弦张力红外发散的长程Nambu-Goldstone玻色子分布，且低频振荡时NGB辐射被强烈抑制，因此其引力波信号可能与局域弦相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局U(1)弦因长程Nambu-Goldstone玻色子导致弦张力红外发散，而斯格明子线和半局域弦作为红外有限弦，其引力波产生机制尚不明确，需厘清NGB辐射是否显著影响其可观测性。

Method: 通过分析红外有限宇宙弦（斯格明子线、半局域弦）的低频振荡动力学，结合Nambu-Goldstone玻色子传播特性，论证其NGB辐射被强烈压制。

Result: 斯格明子线和半局域弦的NGB辐射在低频振荡下被强烈抑制，因而其引力波信号强度可与局域弦相媲美，而非被NGB辐射主导或削弱。

Conclusion: 红外有限宇宙弦虽无长程NGB剖面，但其引力波信号不可忽略，是未来引力波探测的重要候选源；该结论拓展了宇宙弦引力波模板库，并提示需重新评估相关模型的观测约束。

Abstract: Skyrmion lines and semi-local strings are examples of infrared-finite cosmic strings that arise in theories with spontaneously broken global symmetries. Unlike global $U(1)$ strings, they do not support the long-range Nambu-Goldstone boson (NGB) profile responsible for a logarithmically infrared-divergent string tension. We argue that NGB emission from their low-frequency oscillations is strongly suppressed. Consequently, their gravitational-wave signals may be comparable to those from local strings.

</details>


### [99] [Comparison of pair-source and correlation-function based Lévy analyses in Ar+Sc system at SPS energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08141)
*Barnabás Pórfy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用Lévy稳定分布超越高斯近似，结合UrQMD模拟对Ar+Sc中心碰撞中的π介子源开展三维束流动力学（femtoscopy）分析，系统提取并研究源参数随横质量与碰撞能量的演化，为NA61/SHINE实验提供强子唯象基准，并对比了三维关联函数FFT重构法与源分布投影法在参数提取上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯特征已在高能碰撞的束流动力学观测量中被观测到，但其物理起源尚未完全明确；同时需为NA61/SHINE等实验建立可靠的强子层次唯象基准。

Method: 采用Lévy-stable源分布替代传统高斯假设，基于UrQMD模型模拟进行三维pion源分析；对三维对关联函数采用快速傅里叶变换（FFT）重建，并与三维源分布投影方法提取的参数进行直接比较。

Result: 成功提取了Lévy参数（如α、λ）随横质量及碰撞能量的变化趋势；明确了两种参数提取方法（投影法 vs FFT重构法）的系统性差异；为NA61/SHINE实验提供了可区分强子与可能新物理贡献的基准。

Conclusion: Lévy稳定分布是描述非高斯束流动力学现象的有效工具；UrQMD框架下获得的强子源参数具有明确的运动学依赖性；方法学对比揭示了分析流程对结果的影响，强调需统一分析范式以提升跨实验比较的可靠性。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian features in femtoscopic observables have been observed in high-energy collisions, yet their origin remains not fully understood. This study focuses on the phenomenological approach to femtoscopy, going beyond the Gaussian approximation by utilizing Lévy-stable source distributions. Using UrQMD simulations, three-dimensional analyses of pion sources are done in central Ar+Sc collisions, and the extracted parameters are studied as functions of transverse mass and collision energy. This analysis contributes to a hadronic baseline motivated by past and future research work in femtoscopy at the NA61/SHINE experiment. A direct methodological comparison is presented between parameters extracted from the projections of three-dimensional pair-source distribution and those obtained from the three-dimensional correlation function, reconstructed via Fast Fourier Transform.

</details>


### [100] [Relations between electroweak parameters to order $α_s^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08282)
*Andreas Maier,Peter Marquard*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了顶夸克介导的四圈QCD修正对电弱参数Δr和Δκ的影响，为未来轻子对撞机（如FCC-ee）提供更高精度的理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电弱参数测量精度不断提高，未来轻子对撞机需要更精确的理论预言，尤其是高阶QCD修正。

Method: 通过在MZ² ≪ mt²极限下展开带电与中性规范玻色子的真空极化函数（保留六项），计算顶夸克主导的四圈QCD修正对Δr和Δκ的贡献。

Result: 得到对W玻色子质量约数百keV的修正，该效应在FCC-ee实验中具有数值重要性；截断误差可忽略。

Conclusion: 四圈QCD修正不可忽略，必须纳入未来高精度电弱精密测量的理论分析中。

Abstract: Relations between electroweak parameters can be measured with ever-increasing precision and a future lepton collider will require major advances in the theoretical predictions. We derive top-quark mediated four-loop QCD corrections to $Δr$ and $Δκ$, that is to the relations between the weak boson masses, the electroweak couplings, and the weak mixing angle. To this end, we expand the vacuum polarization functions of charged and neutral gauge bosons in $M_Z^2 \ll m_t^2$, finding a negligible truncation error after six terms. Our results imply a shift of a few hundred keV in the W boson mass, which will be numerically relevant for measurements at the FCC-ee.

</details>


### [101] [Compositeness of near-threshold exotic hadrons in systems with Coulomb and short-range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08340)
*Tomona Kinugawa,Tetsuo Hyodo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了库仑加短程相互作用系统中近阈值s波本征态的普适性质，通过分析其组分性，发现长程库仑相互作用会改变纯短程系统的低能普适性阈值行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解库仑与短程相互作用共同作用下近阈值束缚态或共振态的普适行为，特别是其组分性如何受库仑效应调制。

Method: 采用非相对论有效场论方法，推导近阈值态的组分性与库仑散射长度、库仑有效程及玻尔半径之间的解析关系。

Result: 发现弱库仑情形下仍保留短程相互作用主导的低能普适性，对应组分主导的近阈值态；而强库仑情形下该普适性残余消失。

Conclusion: 库仑相互作用显著修正纯短程系统的低能普适阈值行为，其影响程度取决于库仑与短程相互作用的相对强度。

Abstract: We study the universal properties of near-threshold $s$-wave eigenstates in systems with Coulomb plus short-range interactions by analyzing their compositeness. Using a nonrelativistic effective field theory, we derive a relation between the compositeness of near-threshold states and the Coulomb scattering length, the Coulomb effective range, and the Bohr radius. We find that the long-range Coulomb interaction modifies the threshold behavior expected from the low-energy universality of purely short-range systems. When the Coulomb interaction is relatively weak compared with the short-range interaction, a remnant of the low-energy universality associated with the short-range interaction survives, leading to composite-dominant near-threshold eigenstates. In contrast, in the strong-Coulomb regime, this remnant disappears.

</details>


### [102] [Medium modification of pseudoscalar meson structure: Distribution amplitudes and parton distribution functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08360)
*Muhammad Ridwan,Parada Tobel Paraduan Hutauruk,Ahmad Jafar Arifi,Kazuo Tsushima*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在轻前夸克模型中结合夸克-介子耦合模型，研究了π、K、D、B等赝标量介子在核介质中的分布振幅和部分子分布函数，发现介质显著改变其结构，特别是π介子的Gegenbauer系数a₂明显增大，并呈现出类似EMC效应的PDF比行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用下介子在核介质中的内部结构演化，尤其是分布振幅与部分子分布函数如何受介质影响，是连接非微扰QCD与核物理的关键问题。

Method: 采用轻前夸克模型（LFQM），结合高斯型与幂律型波函数，并嵌入来自夸克-介子耦合（QMC）模型的介质中夸克性质；计算对称核物质中π±、K±、D±、B±介子的分布振幅（DA）与部分子分布函数（PDF），分析Gegenbauer系数、Mellin矩及PDF比Rρ/0π。

Result: 介质中π介子DA在中间x区显著压低；a₂^π随核密度升高明显增大（高斯/幂律波函数下分别从0.096/0.057增至0.232/0.202）；PDF比Rρ/0π呈现类EMC效应的x依赖；自由空间DA与格点QCD结果基本一致。

Conclusion: 核介质显著重塑赝标量介子的纵向动量结构，尤其增强高阶Gegenbauer分量，表明其内部波函数更宽展；QCD演化在介质效应中起关键作用；该统一夸克图像为理解EMC效应提供了新视角。

Abstract: In this work, we present a comprehensive investigation of the in-medium properties and internal structure of pseudoscalar mesons within the light-front quark model (LFQM), employing both Gaussian and power-law (PL) wave functions. We incorporate in-medium quark properties obtained from the quark-meson coupling (QMC) model into the LFQM, thereby providing a unified description in terms of quark degrees of freedom. We investigate the distribution amplitudes (DAs) and parton distribution functions (PDFs) of $π^{\pm}$, $K^{\pm}$, $D^{\pm}$, and $B^{\pm}$ mesons in symmetric nuclear matter. We find that the evolved in-medium pion DA and PDF are strongly suppressed at intermediate $x$, indicating substantial medium-induced modifications to the meson structure. The signs and magnitudes of the modified Gegenbauer coefficients $a_n'$, the parameter $ξ$, and the Mellin moments of the DAs and PDFs in nuclear matter are found to be consistent with other theoretical predictions. In particular, the second Gegenbauer coefficient of the pion DA, $a_2^π$, increases substantially with nuclear density, from $0.096$ ($0.057$) in vacuum to $0.232$ ($0.202$) at normal nuclear density for the Gaussian (PL) wave function, respectively. This increase indicates a broader pion DA and an enhanced second Gegenbauer moment in the nuclear medium. We further calculate the pion PDF ratio $R_{ρ/0}^π$ and find that its $x$ dependence exhibits a behavior qualitatively similar to the observed European Muon Collaboration (EMC) effect, highlighting the important role of QCD evolution in shaping the medium-modified partonic structure. Finally, we compare the calculated free-space $π^{\pm}$ and $K^{\pm}$ DAs with available free-space lattice QCD results and find reasonable agreement.

</details>


### [103] [Exploring asymmetries in three-body cLFV lepton decays: probing CP violation in HNL extensions of the SM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08366)
*Adrian Darricau,Jonathan Kriewald,Ana M. Teixeira*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了标准模型扩展中马约拉纳无菌费米子对带电轻子味破坏（cLFV）过程的影响，特别关注极化μ和τ介子三体衰变中的角观测量及宇称、时间反演不对称性，并探讨其作为新物理探针的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含马约拉纳无菌费米子的标准模型扩展中，额外的狄拉克与马约拉纳CP破坏相可能显著影响带电轻子味破坏（cLFV）过程，需系统研究其可观测效应。

Method: 分析极化μ和τ介子的三体cLFV衰变ℓα⁺ → ℓβ⁺ℓγ⁺ℓδ⁻中的角分布、宇称不对称性和时间反演不对称性，并研究不同cLFV观测量之间的关联。

Result: 在实验可及的参数空间内可产生显著的不对称性；角观测量的特征模式可有效区分并检验此类新物理模型。

Conclusion: 角观测量是探测含马约拉纳无菌费米子的标准模型扩展的有力工具，若未来观测到cLFV信号，可用于检验或证伪该理论框架。

Abstract: In extensions of the Standard Model with Majorana sterile fermions, additional Dirac and Majorana CP-violating phases can have a significant impact on charged lepton flavour violating (cLFV) transitions and decays. We investigate angular observables associated with polarised $μ$ and $τ$ cLFV three-body decays, $\ell_α^+ \to \ell_β^+ \ell_γ^+ \ell_δ^-$, including parity- and time-reversal asymmetries. We analyse the correlations between different cLFV observables, showing that sizeable asymmetries can arise over experimentally accessible regions of parameter space. The resulting patterns of angular observables provide powerful probes of this class of Standard Model extensions and, in the event of a cLFV signal, could offer a means to test or potentially falsify the underlying framework.

</details>


### [104] [Energy-momentum tensor at small $x$ from gravitational transverse-momentum distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08456)
*Tomoya Uji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了小-x胶子和海夸克的动量空间力学性质，引入了引力横向动量分布（gravitational TMDs），建立了其与常规TMD的相关性，并在MV模型和BK演化框架下进行了数值计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解小-x区域强子内部胶子和海夸克的力学结构，特别是其动量密度、惯性流和应力等机械性质，弥补传统TMD对力学信息描述的不足。

Method: 基于光前规范不变的正则能量-动量张量构建胶子引力TMD；推导其与普通TMD关联；在偶极近似和eikonal近似下解析分析；采用McLerran-Venugopalan初始条件与固定耦合LO BK演化进行数值计算；利用高横动量区的扭三夸克TMD计算海夸克惯性横向流。

Result: 明确了胶子纵向与横向正则动量密度由无极化分布决定，而惯性横向流与横向应力在eikonal近似下消失；数值结果揭示了正则横动量与惯性流有效速度之间偏离自由粒子关系的现象。

Conclusion: 引力TMD为小-x QCD提供了新的力学观测量；胶子与海夸克的机械性质可通过TMD框架定量刻画，且显著偏离自由粒子图像，反映强相互作用下的集体动力学效应。

Abstract: We investigate the momentum-space mechanical properties of small-$x$ gluons and sea quarks using gravitational transverse-momentum distributions (gravitational TMDs). For gluons, we formulate these distributions using the light-front gauge-invariant canonical energy-momentum tensor and derive their relations to ordinary TMD correlators, identifying momentum densities, the transverse flux of inertia, and transverse stresses. In the eikonal approximation, the longitudinal and transverse canonical gluon momentum densities are determined by the unpolarized distribution, whereas the transverse flux of inertia and transverse stresses vanish. For an unpolarized target, we numerically evaluate the gluon and sea-quark momentum densities using a common initial condition based on the McLerran--Venugopalan model and fixed-coupling leading-order Balitsky--Kovchegov evolution. We also evaluate the sea-quark transverse flux of inertia through the twist-three quark TMD at the high transverse-momentum region. These results illustrate departures from the free-particle relation between canonical transverse momentum and the effective transverse velocity governing inertia flow.

</details>


### [105] [Missing Mass Matters: Neutrinophilic Scalar in $D$-Meson Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08606)
*Jin-Man Cai,Gang Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了中微子亲和标量φ在D介子衰变中的发射，通过BESIII和未来超级陶粲工厂（STCF）实验探测该粒子，并修正了以往基于积分三体衰变率的限制，发现新方法对较重标量更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索轻中微子亲和标量φ（主要耦合到中微子）在10 MeV至约100 MeV质量区间内的实验可探性，特别是通过介子衰变过程。

Method: 分析D介子（D⁺和Dₛ⁺）衰变到e⁺ν̄αφ的过程，考虑缺失质量平方M²ₘᵢₛₛ的分布及其在实验选区内的依赖性；推导包含标量与带电轻子质量全依赖关系的三体衰变宽度解析表达式；评估BESIII与STCF实验的灵敏度。

Result: D⁺衰变的新约束比以往基于积分衰变率的估计显著减弱；STCF在1 ab⁻¹积分亮度下，对mφ ≳ 380 MeV（D⁺）和mφ ≳ 310 MeV（Dₛ⁺）的灵敏度将超越现有K介子实验限制；还讨论了μ⁺末态的直接搜寻潜力。

Conclusion: 利用完整M²ₘᵢₛₛ运动学范围可大幅提升对中微子亲和标量的探测能力，尤其在较高质量区域；本文提供的解析衰变宽度公式为未来相关理论与实验分析提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: A light neutrinophilic scalar $φ$, which couples predominantly to neutrinos, can be probed through leptonic meson decays for scalar masses $10~\mathrm{MeV}\lesssim m_φ\lesssim\mathcal{O}(100)~\mathrm{MeV}$. We study neutrinophilic-scalar emission in $D$-meson decays at BESIII and the future Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF), focusing on $D_{(s)}^+\to e^+\barν_αφ$. Accounting for the $m_φ$-dependent missing-mass squared ($M_{\rm miss}^2$) distribution within the experimentally selected region, we find the resulting $D^+$ constraint to be substantially weaker than previous estimates based on the integrated three-body decay rate. Extending the $M_{\rm miss}^2$ coverage provides sensitivity to heavier scalars. With the full kinematic range of $M_{\rm miss}^2$ and an integrated luminosity of $1~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, the projected STCF sensitivities surpass the existing kaon bound for $m_φ\gtrsim 380~\mathrm{MeV}$ in the $D^+$ decay and $m_φ\gtrsim 310~\mathrm{MeV}$ in the $D_s^+$ decay. We further discuss direct searches for $D_{(s)}^+\toμ^+\barν_αφ$ and their potential for probing the neutrinophilic scalar. A complete analytic expression for the three-body decay width is also derived with full dependence on the scalar and charged-lepton masses, including its behavior in the $m_φ\to 0$ and $m_\ell\to 0$ limits.

</details>


### [106] [Testing Dark Matter Coscattering with the High-Energy LZ Recoil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08648)
*Sreemanti Chakraborti,Soumyajit Datta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究LZ实验观测到的高能核反冲是否能探测由coscattering或转换驱动冻结机制决定暗物质丰度的模型；提出一个单态-二重态费米子暗物质模型，通过轻暗光子增强端吸热跃迁，使LZ实验可探测该机制主导的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LZ实验观测到的高能核反冲能否探测由coscattering或转换驱动冻结机制决定暗物质丰度的物理场景，特别是验证同一相互作用在宇宙学早期（决定丰度）和当前直接探测实验中的可观测性。

Method: 构建含轻暗光子中介的单态-二重态费米子暗物质模型，其中暗物质主为单态、与二重态存在约100 keV质量差；在小混合角下，暗物质通过χ₁N→χ₂N端吸热跃迁实现coscattering机制控制丰度；利用暗光子增强该跃迁截面，提升直接探测信号。

Result: 当暗光子质量m_{A'}=250 MeV时，LZ实验敏感区几乎完全覆盖符合遗迹丰度要求的参数空间（含主导coscattering区域）；随m_{A'}增大，LZ敏感区向更大混合角偏移，与coscattering区重叠减小；结合遗迹密度与核反冲约束，选出暗物质质量范围约为750 GeV–1.6 TeV。

Conclusion: LZ实验的高能核反冲信号可直接检验决定暗物质遗迹丰度的核心转换机制，实现对同一物理过程在宇宙不同时期（早期热历史与现今直接探测）的一致性检验。

Abstract: We investigate whether the high-energy nuclear recoil recently reported by LZ can probe dark matter (DM) whose relic abundance is set by coscattering, or conversion-driven freeze-out. We consider a singlet-doublet fermion DM model coupled to a light dark photon, in which the predominantly singlet-like DM state is separated from a more strongly interacting doublet-like state by a $\sim 100\,{\rm keV}$ mass splitting. In the small mixing regime, conversion into the doublet sector becomes the bottleneck controlling the relic abundance, realising coscattering, while the same small mixing would ordinarily suppress direct detection. A light dark photon enhances the endothermic transition $χ_1 N\toχ_2 N$, allowing the conversion-driven region to be probed by high-energy nuclear recoils. We find that for $m_{A'}=250~{\rm MeV}$ the LZ-sensitive region can cover essentially the full relic-compatible parameter space, including the dominantly conversion-driven regime. As the mediator mass is increased, the LZ sensitivity progressively shifts towards larger singlet-doublet mixing, reducing its overlap with the coscattering region. Combining the relic density and recoil requirements selects DM masses extending approximately from $750~{\rm GeV}$ to $1.6~{\rm TeV}$ for the mediator masses considered. The LZ recoil can therefore directly test the same interaction and physical parameters that dominantly set the DM relic abundance, providing a probe of the same conversion mechanism across vastly different cosmological epochs.

</details>


### [107] [Dark matter in the x-channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08724)
*Luca Panizzi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型暗物质模型（x-通道），通过引入携带非平凡量子数的标量中介粒子，使希格斯门户相互作用（包括圈图）被强烈压制；该机制依赖于希格斯门户耦合在零动量转移处的边界条件，确保树图与单圈贡献消失；中介粒子不与希格斯混合，仅与新物理态耦合；最小实现中中介为带电无色SU(2)_L单态标量，其衰变至轻子；研究了对撞机、希格斯物理、味物理及天体/宇宙学约束的互补性；存活参数空间主要对应暗物质质量大于中介粒子，LHC限制设定了该区域下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准希格斯门户暗物质模型中难以避免的强约束问题，特别是当希格斯-暗物质耦合即使在圈图级别也需被显著压制时，探索一种机制上自洽且现象学可行的新框架。

Method: 构建基于标量单态暗物质与新型标量中介粒子（如带电SU(2)_L单态）的理论模型；分析其量子数结构如何阻止希格斯混合与直接门户耦合；利用重整化群演化和边界条件论证Higgs portal耦合在零动量下的消失；系统扫描参数空间，结合LHC、Higgs信号、味物理（如μ→eγ）、以及暗物质丰度与间接探测等多方面实验约束进行联合分析。

Result: 发现满足所有当前实验约束的参数点主要集中于暗物质质量大于带电标量中介粒子的区域；LHC对带电标量的直接搜寻设定了该区域的下限；部分剩余参数空间可被未来对撞机（如HL-LHC或FCC）通过中介粒子产生过程覆盖；该x-通道机制具有普适性，其他中介选择可导向不同唯象方向。

Conclusion: x-通道提供了一种规避传统Higgs portal强约束的新范式，其核心在于量子数设计与耦合边界条件的协同；它不仅拓展了暗物质模型的理论可能性，也为多信使实验探测提供了新的目标和策略；后续工作可推广至更广义的中介表示及耦合结构。

Abstract: In this paper I study a class of scenarios in which a scalar singlet dark matter candidate interacts with the Standard Model in such a way that the Higgs portal is strongly suppressed, even at loop level, through a specific mechanism involving scalar mediators. The mediator carries non-trivial gauge or internal quantum numbers that allow only a quartic coupling with the dark matter, and belongs to representations with no components that can mix with the Higgs boson, so that the dark matter interacts only with exotic states. I refer to this construction as $x$-channel because no single mediator is exchanged between the dark and visible sectors, to distinguish it from the standard $s$- and $t$-channel simplified scenarios. The suppression is due to a boundary condition on the running Higgs-portal coupling, which makes the tree-level and one-loop Higgs-mediated contributions vanish at zero momentum transfer, regardless of the internal quantum numbers of the mediator. I consider here a minimal realisation, in which the mediator is a singly- or doubly-charged colourless $SU(2)_L$-singlet scalar decaying to leptons, and study the complementarity between collider, Higgs, flavour, and astrophysical/cosmological constraints. Within the scanned ranges and benchmark assumptions, the surviving points are predominantly in the region where the dark matter is heavier than the charged scalar mediator. LHC limits determine the lower edge of this region, part of which can be probed by future colliders through the production of the charged scalars. Other realisations of the $x$-channel can open different phenomenological directions to explore.

</details>

### [108] [And Then There WEre Baryons (ATWEB): The Baryon Budget of the Universe in Halos and the IGM over 13 Billion Years of Cosmic Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07140)
*Mohammadreza Ayromlou,G. Mark Voit,Thomas H. Reiprich,Cristiano Porciani,Dominique Eckert*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过TNG、EAGLE和SIMBA模拟研究了宇宙中重子物质与暗物质分布的偏离现象，引入了补偿半径、闭合包络和补偿包络等新特征尺度，揭示了重子循环、IGM富集及反馈对大尺度结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测显示本地宇宙中除最庞大星系团外，晕内重子分数低于宇宙平均值，需理解其偏离机制与时空演化规律。

Method: 利用TNG、EAGLE和SIMBA三种先进宇宙学流体动力学模拟，分析z=0及演化过程中重子与暗物质的空间分布差异，定义并追踪多个特征半径与包络尺度。

Result: 发现z=0时仅<30%宇宙重子位于星系宿主晕内；提出补偿半径（总质量恢复引力唯象值处）、闭合包络与补偿包络；星系群主导IGM富集与重子再分配，闭合包络达2–6.5 Mpc；补偿包络（1.5–5.5 Mpc）与物质功率谱恢复引力形式的尺度一致。

Conclusion: 重子再分配是普遍且动态的过程，存在开放的重子循环；反馈效应不仅改变晕内结构，还显著影响数兆秒差距尺度的大尺度结构，补偿包络为连接星系形成与宇宙学结构演化的关键桥梁。

Abstract: Observations of the local Universe show that, except in the most massive galaxy clusters, the baryon fraction within halos falls below the cosmic mean. This deficit can be characterized using the previously established closure radius, the halocentric distance within which the enclosed baryon fraction reaches the cosmic value. Using the TNG, EAGLE and SIMBA simulations, we examine how, when, and on what scales the baryonic and dark matter distributions diverge. At z=0 less than 30% of cosmic baryons reside within galaxy-hosting halos, compared with ~45% of the dark matter, leaving over 70% of all baryons in the IGM. Building on the closure radius, we introduce three further characteristic scales: the compensation radius, closure envelope, and compensation envelope. The compensation radius marks where the enclosed total mass recovers its gravity-only value, and today it typically lies inside the closure radius in all three models. The closure and compensation envelopes are the largest median radii across halo mass. Galaxy groups provide the largest contribution per dex of halo mass to IGM enrichment with halo-associated baryons and redistribute baryons over the largest distances. They set the closure envelope, which reaches ~2-6.5 Mpc at z=0, depending on the model. The closure radius usually increases with cosmic time at fixed halo mass. Tracking individual halos, we find open baryon cycles with substantial redistribution beyond halo boundaries across all halo masses, while even the progenitors of today's most massive clusters fall below the cosmic baryon fraction. The compensation envelope grows toward low redshift, reaching ~1.5-5.5 Mpc at z=0. Across cosmic time, it coincides with the scale on which the matter power spectrum recovers its gravity-only form, establishing a direct link between the spatial extent of baryon redistribution and the impact of feedback on large-scale structure.

</details>


### [109] [Lensing in the Blue IV: The First Weak Gravitational Lensing Maps from the Stratosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07244)
*Sayan Saha,Maya Amit,Jacqueline E. McCleary,Georgios N. Vassilakis,Emaad Paracha,Giulia Cerini,Spencer W. Everett,Leila Ohashi,Eric M. Huff,Gavin Leroy,Leo W. H. Fung,Richard Massey,Steven J. Benton,William C. Jones,Thuy Vy T. Luu,Ajay S. Gill,Mohamed M. Shaaban,Philippe Voyer,Adam Field,Adam Aoua,Anthony M. Brown,Paul Clark,Matthew Craigie,Christopher J. Damaren,Tim Eifler,David Harvey,Eric Habjan,John W. Hartley,Bradley Holder,Mathilde Jauzac,David Lagattuta,Jason S. -Y. Leung,Lun Li,Johanna M. Nagy,C. Barth Netterfield,Susan F. Redmond,Jason D. Rhodes,Andrew Robertson,L. Javier Romualdez,Jürgen Schmoll,Ellen Sirks,Sut Ieng Tam,Karuna Tarafdar,André Z. Vitorelli,Alfredo Zenteno*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用SuperBIT气球望远镜2023年科学飞行获取的6个星系团数据，首次实现了亚轨道天文观测平台上的弱引力透镜质量绘图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证亚轨道平台（如超压气球）能否实现高质量弱引力透镜观测与质量重建，拓展地面与空间望远镜之外的观测能力。

Method: 基于SuperBIT三波段测光构建背景星系样本，采用颜色-颜色方法分离前景/背景星系；利用已校准的星系形状（Lensing in the Blue III），通过Kaiser-Squires反演法重建弱透镜汇聚场。

Result: 成功生成6个星系团的弱透镜汇聚图，并与X射线辐射、红序成员星系数密度图等独立探针一致，证实亚轨道平台可开展可靠弱透镜质量测绘。

Conclusion: SuperBIT首次在亚轨道平台上实现了成功的弱引力透镜质量绘图，为未来低成本、高分辨率弱透镜巡天提供了新途径。

Abstract: The Super-Pressure Balloon-borne Imaging Telescope (SuperBIT) completed its science flight in April 2023, obtaining imaging data for 30 galaxy clusters. In this work, we present the first weak-lensing convergence maps from these observations, using six clusters selected from the 30 targets. We construct background-galaxy catalogs using SuperBIT's three-band photometry and a color-color-based method to separate foreground and background galaxies, and use the calibrated galaxy shapes presented in Lensing in the Blue III to reconstruct the weak-lensing convergence field using Kaiser-Squires inversion. We compare the convergence signal-to-noise maps for the six clusters with independent tracers, including X-ray emission and red-sequence cluster-member number-density maps. These results demonstrate the first successful weak-lensing mass mapping with imaging from a sub-orbital astronomical observatory.

</details>


### [110] [Stochastic Bubble Completion in de Sitter Space: Power Spectrum, Bispectrum, and Infrared Moment Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07880)
*Hyukjung Kim,Alp Kagan Ozmen,Heeseung Zoe*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在一阶相变中，由于随机气泡成核导致的局部完成时间作为随机空间场的功率谱和等边三谱特性，特别关注有限相变持续时间带来的红外效应。在永恒德西特背景中，远古气泡尾部引发红外敏感的空间矩；而在有限起始时间或时间局域化情形下，所有矩保持有限，并恢复白噪声极限谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一阶相变中完成时间涨落的统计特性，尤其是其在德西特时空中的红外行为，对宇宙学曲率扰动与非高斯性具有重要意义。

Method: 理论分析结合数值计算，研究Minkowski与de Sitter背景下的完成时间随机场的功率谱与等边三谱，重点考察古代气泡尾部对红外行为的影响及不同背景（永恒 vs 有限起始）下的阈值行为。

Result: 发现二点函数与三点函数分别在H/β=1/3、1/6处出现奇点，在H/β=1/5、1/8处首项有限k修正变为尾部敏感；有限起始时间或时间局域化可消除奇点并恢复白噪声极限P_τ∝k³与B^eq_τ∝k⁶。

Conclusion: 建立了适用于完成时间涨落的通用首达框架，阐明了德西特膨胀如何产生区别于功率谱的三谱红外层级结构，为宇宙学扰动与非高斯性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Stochastic bubble nucleation makes the local completion time of a first-order phase transition a random spatial field. We study its power spectrum and equilateral bispectrum in Minkowski and de Sitter backgrounds, with particular emphasis on the infrared consequences of a finite transition duration. In an eternally extended de Sitter history, rare bubbles nucleated in the remote past generate an ancient-bubble tail that produces a hierarchy of infrared-sensitive spatial moments. For the two-point function, the first finite-$k$ correction becomes tail sensitive at $H/β=1/5$, while the leading moment becomes singular at $H/β=1/3$. The corresponding three-point thresholds are $H/β=1/8$ and $1/6$, showing that the bispectrum is more infrared sensitive than the power spectrum. For any finite starting time, however, the ancient-bubble tail is cut off, all spatial moments remain finite, and the strict infrared spectra recover the analytic white-noise limits ${\cal P}_τ\propto k^3$ and ${\cal B}^{\rm eq}_τ\propto k^6$. The eternal-background thresholds instead control the duration dependence of the moment coefficients and the crossover into an intermediate tail-sensitive regime, in agreement with our numerical calculations. We further show that temporal localization of the nucleation history provides a smooth regulator of the ancient-bubble tail and restores the same analytic infrared limits. These results establish a general first-arrival framework for completion-time fluctuations and clarify how de Sitter expansion can generate distinct power-spectrum and bispectrum infrared hierarchies relevant to curvature perturbations and non-Gaussianity.

</details>


### [111] [Fast and accurate differentiable code of the galaxy power spectrum based on Eulerian and Lagrangian one-loop perturbation theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08023)
*Yosuke Kobayashi,Kazuyuki Akitsu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了elf，一个基于JAX-Python实现的、支持自动微分的一阶圈图精度星系功率谱计算代码，适用于欧拉和拉格朗日扰动理论（EPT/LPT），在GPU上毫秒级运行，误差低至0.01%，并首次实现了LPT下可自动微分的一阶圈图功率谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持宇宙学参数推断（如HMC采样）所需的高效、高精度且可自动微分的星系功率谱计算，特别是针对LPT中长波位移自然重求和带来的优势（如处理原初特征等超出BAO的结构）。

Method: 基于JAX实现EPT和LPT框架下一阶圈图精度的星系功率谱计算，利用JIT编译与自动微分；系统分析并最小化离散化等数值误差。

Result: 代码可在GPU上几毫秒内完成EPT/LPT功率谱计算，相对误差约0.01%（k ≲ 1 h Mpc⁻¹）；首次实现LPT下可自动微分的一阶圈图功率谱；支持含原初特征等非标准BAO结构的应用。

Conclusion: elf是首个支持LPT一阶圈图功率谱自动微分的开源工具，兼具高精度、高效率与强适用性，显著提升了EFTofLSS在贝叶斯参数推断及复杂功率谱建模中的实用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a JAX-Python implementation of the galaxy power spectrum up to one-loop order in the effective field theory of large-scale structure, based on both the Eulerian and Lagrangian perturbation theories. The code uses JAX to enable automatic differentiation with respect to parameters such as cosmology, galaxy bias, or counterterm coefficients. This facilitates applications such as parameter inference using the gradient of the posterior, e.g., Hamiltonian Monte Carlo sampling. The code can compute both the EPT and LPT power spectra in a few milliseconds on a GPU, after just-in-time compilation. We also identify the various sources of numerical errors associated with discretization, and design the numerical implementation to minimize them, achieving relative errors of order $0.01\%$ at scales $k \lesssim 1\, h\, \mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$, compared with brute-force numerical integration. Notably, this is the first auto-differentiable implementation of the one-loop galaxy power spectrum based on LPT. The natural resummation of the long-wavelength displacements in LPT further makes the code well suited for applications involving features in the power spectrum beyond the standard BAO feature, including primordial features. Our code, elf, is publicly available.

</details>


### [112] [Third-order scalar induced gravitational waves during a matter-dominated era: secular growth and implications for primordial black hole domination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08045)
*Jing-Zhi Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了物质主导时期（MD）中三阶标量诱导引力波（SIGWs），首次解析得到了四类源的核函数，并发现其谱增长比辐射主导时期更快；数值计算表明，三阶效应显著增强对原初黑洞（PBH）丰度的约束，比仅考虑二阶时强约三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在早期宇宙物质主导时期，高阶标量诱导引力波可能显著增强并导致非微扰效应，但此前缺乏系统分析，尤其缺少三阶贡献的完整解析与数值研究；同时，PBH主导的早期MD era中，SIGWs的回授效应可能严重限制PBH丰度，需更高阶修正以获得可靠约束。

Method: 解析推导了物质主导时期三阶SIGWs的四类源（含三一阶标量、一阶标量×二阶张量/矢量/标量）对应的核函数，全部得到闭式解；将三阶功率谱表达为五重积分，并针对单色原初谱进行数值计算；结合PBH驱动的早期MD模型，利用总引力波能量密度积分约束∫dlnk Ω^tot_GW < 1，推导PBH初始丰度上限。

Result: 发现MD时期的三阶核函数具有长期增长行为，导致Ω^(3)_GW ∝ x^6（x = kη ≫ 1），远快于二阶的∝ x^2；单色谱下二阶矢量源贡献严格为零；应用于PBH主导MD era时，三阶修正使PBH丰度约束达Ω_PBH,f ≲ 4.6×10⁻⁷ (m_PBH/10⁷ g)⁻⁰·⁴⁸，比二阶结果强约三个数量级。

Conclusion: 三阶SIGWs在足够长的物质主导时期不可避免地进入非微扰区域，因此在涉及早期物质主导的宇宙学场景（如PBH主导时期、跷跷板机制、模场衰变等）中，必须纳入高阶效应，否则将严重低估引力波产额及相应观测或稳定性约束。

Abstract: We investigate scalar induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) at third-order during a matter-dominated (MD) era. We classify the sources into four parts---built from three first-order scalars, and from a first-order scalar times the second-order tensor, vector, and scalar perturbations---and obtain all four kernel functions analytically in closed form. Unlike their radiation-dominated counterparts, the MD kernel functions grow secularly, translating into a rapid growth of the spectrum, $Ω^{(3)}_{\mathrm{GW}}\propto x^{6}$ versus $Ω^{(2)}_{\mathrm{GW}}\propto x^{2}$ at $x\equiv kη\gg1$. We derive the third-order power spectrum as a five-fold integral for a general primordial spectrum, and evaluate it numerically for a monochromatic one, where all contributions from the second-order vector source vanish identically. As an application, we consider the early MD era driven by primordial black holes (PBHs) before their evaporation. Requiring that the induced gravitational waves do not backreact on the cosmological perturbations, $\int\mathrm{d}\ln k\, Ω^{\mathrm{tot}}_{\mathrm{GW}}<1$ then bounds the initial PBH abundance as $Ω_{\mathrm{PBH},f}\lesssim4.6\times10^{-7}\, (m_{\mathrm{PBH}}/10^{7}\,\mathrm{g})^{-0.48}$, about three orders of magnitude stronger than the second-order-only bound. Our results indicate that higher-order SIGWs inevitably become nonperturbative during a sufficiently long MD era, with important implications for scenarios involving an early matter domination.

</details>


### [113] [Probing beyond the Cosmic Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08083)
*Tarun Souradeep*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了宇宙大尺度结构的全局拓扑性质，特别是通过CMB各向异性探测超出视界尺度的非平凡全局结构的可能性，并以椭球拓扑在闭合FRW宇宙中产生显著CMB信号为例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测到的均匀曲率区域可能只是复杂流形中远小于非均匀性和全局连通性尺度的一个微小斑块；而超新星数据暗示视界尺度可能接近甚至远大于曲率半径，因此有必要研究视界外可能存在的非平凡全局结构。

Method: 基于当前超新星观测所支持的闭合FRW宇宙（球面几何）参数空间，分析椭球拓扑等非平凡全局结构对CMB各向异性的理论影响。

Result: 在修正后的闭合FRW宇宙参数空间中，椭球拓扑在很大一部分参数范围内能产生显著的高阶CMB各向异性信号。

Conclusion: CMB各向异性是探测视界尺度乃至略超视界尺度上宇宙全局拓扑结构的敏感探针，有望揭示或限制非平凡的宇宙全局结构。

Abstract: There are reasons to believe that the region of uniform curvature that we observe within our horizon is perhaps a tiny patch, much smaller than the length scales of inhomogeneity and global connectivity of an extremely complicated manifold. However, the recent supernova (SN) results suggest that the horizon scale could be comparable to or even much larger than curvature radius. Non trivial global structure too tends to be of the order of the local curvature scale. Probing (slightly) beyond the cosmic horizon can potentially reveal nontrivial global structure lurking around or just beyond the horizon scale. The cosmic microwave background (CMB) anisotropy is a sensitive probe of the universe on length scales up to and somewhats beyond the horizon scale and is perhaps poised to detect or put interesting limits on nontrivial features the global structure. This point is exemplified by high CMB anisotropy signal of the Elliptical topology in a large fraction of the revised parameter space of the closed FRW universe (spherical geometry) currently preferred by SN observations.

</details>


### [114] [Evolution of neutrino distributions under repeated electron scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08201)
*Jens Chluba,Bryce Cyr,Michiru Uwabo-Niibo,Masahide Yamaguchi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了中微子与自由电子热浴多次弹性散射导致的能量再分布问题，提出了基于散射核的演化方程，并推导了Fokker-Planck极限，对比了光子Kompaneets方程，分析了中微子谱畸变类型及相对论性Sunyaev-Zeldovich效应的类比形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中微子在宇宙学和天体物理环境中因多次弹性散射导致的能量再分布行为，特别是其与光子Compton散射的异同，以及由此产生的谱畸变特征。

Method: 从Boltzmann碰撞积分出发，构建基于中微子-电子再分布核的演化方程；独立推导Fokker-Planck极限；对比Kompaneets方程；分析费米子对应的温度型、化学势型和y型谱畸变；推导Doppler主导下的相对论散射算符；建立保守数值格式并开展线注入实验验证。

Result: 揭示了弱相互作用截面能量依赖性导致中微子高能部分演化远快于低能部分；给出了FP方程在多种极限下的近似解析解；推导出中微子版相对论Sunyaev-Zeldovich效应的解析表达式；数值验证了扩散近似的适用范围及再分布核非局域性的重要场景。

Conclusion: 中微子能量再分布具有显著不同于光子的动力学特性，需采用包含 recoil、相对论运动学和Pauli阻塞的散射核或其FP近似进行精确建模；扩散近似在部分情形下有效，但在关键非局域区域仍需完整核计算。

Abstract: We study the energy redistribution of neutrinos by repeated elastic scattering with an isotropic thermal bath of free electrons. Starting from the relevant Boltzmann collision integral, we formulate the evolution equations in terms of the neutrino--electron redistribution kernel derived in our companion work. The scattering kernel formulation retains recoil, relativistic kinematics and Pauli blocking, while reducing the calculation to a one-dimensional integral in the outgoing neutrino energy. To gain a better analytic understanding, we derive the corresponding Fokker--Planck (FP) limit of the collision operator in two independent ways and contrast it with the Kompaneets equation for photons. This shows that the different energy dependence of the weak cross section changes the drift and diffusion operators which causes the high-energy part of a neutrino distribution to evolve much faster than its low-energy part. We discuss the fermionic analogues of temperature-, chemical-potential- and $y$-type spectral distortions, and give approximate analytic solutions to the FP equation in several limiting cases. We also derive the analytic expressions for the relativistic scattering operator when Doppler terms dominate, allowing us to compute the relativistic Sunyaev-Zeldovich equivalent for neutrinos in the inefficient scattering limit. We compare these expressions to the full numerical result based on the detailed collision term. Finally, we set up conservative numerical schemes for both the FP equation and the scattering kernel, generalizing previous codes developed for studying the repeated Compton scattering of photons by relativistic electrons. Controlled line-injection experiments then illustrate when the diffusion approximation is accurate and where the non-locality of the redistribution kernel remains essential.

</details>


### [115] [ApPolLO: Apertif counterparts of Polarised LOFAR sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08396)
*A. Berger,B. Adebahr,H. Dénes,R. -J. Dettmar*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用LOFAR和Apertif射电偏振数据，分析了598个偏振射电源的去极化比，区分源本地与视线方向上的去极化效应，发现144 MHz去极化主要源于宇宙网丝状结构，1.4 GHz则源于源附近，并首次给出丝状结构中RM弥散的红移依赖上限估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙磁场的结构与演化对揭示原初磁场的产生与放大机制至关重要，而偏振观测是研究宇宙磁场的重要手段。

Method: 结合LOFAR RM格网巡天（144 MHz）与新Apertif数据（1.4 GHz），分析共同样本射电源的偏振谱与去极化特性，计算去极化比，区分源本地与视线方向的去极化贡献，并统计分析其红移依赖性。

Result: 获得包含598个偏振射电源成分的星表；发现偏振分数随红移下降；1.4 GHz主去极化源于源附近，144 MHz主去极化源于视线方向的宇宙丝状结构；首次估算出宇宙网丝状结构中RM弥散的红移依赖上限。

Conclusion: 宇宙磁场探测需联合多频偏振观测以分离不同尺度的去极化效应；低密度环境中射电源的偏振特性支持丝状结构在宇宙磁场演化中起关键作用；该工作为未来高精度宇宙磁场测绘提供了方法学基础与初步约束。

Abstract: Knowledge of the structure and evolution of cosmic magnetic fields is indispensable to understand how primordial magnetic fields were generated and amplified during the lifetime of the Universe. Polarisation studies over significant redshift ranges are an important tool in understanding cosmic magnetic fields. We aim to understand the structure and evolution of cosmic magnetic fields, utilising the polarised spectra and depolarisation characteristics of (background) radio sources. Using radio polarisation data at two different frequencies from the LOFAR Rotation Measure (RM) grid catalogue at 144\,MHz and new Apertif data at 1.4\,GHz, we investigate the source statistics of a common sample of polarised sources, deriving the depolarisation ratio between these two frequencies. We utilise this to disentangle depolarising effects local to the source from those along the line of sight. We compile a catalogue of 598 polarised radio components, most of which are located in low-density environments. Fractional polarisation decrease for both wavelengths with redshift. We attributed the main depolarisation at 1.4\,GHz to effects occurring close to the sources, while the depolarisation measured at 144\,MHz is most likely generated by cosmic filaments along the line-of-sight. We were able to get a first redshift dependent upper limit estimate of the RM dispersion in cosmic web filaments.

</details>


### [116] [The impact of feedback and cosmology on Cosmic Infrared Background cross-correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08436)
*Sara Maleubre,David Alonso,Reggie Dunsdon,Adrien La Posta,Amy Wayland,Joop Schaye*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本研究利用FLAMINGO流体动力学模拟，通过宇宙红外背景（CIB）与大尺度结构示踪物（如星系剪切）的交叉关联，探究恒星形成率密度（ρ_SFR）的演化与成团性，以约束AGN与恒星反馈模型及宇宙学参数。结果表明，当前观测数据显著排除强恒星反馈和强动能型（喷流驱动）AGN反馈模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用CIB与其他大尺度结构示踪物（如宇宙剪切）的交叉关联，来约束AGN与恒星反馈物理模型以及宇宙学参数。

Method: 基于FLAMINGO水动力学模拟，分析不同反馈模型（AGN/恒星反馈）和宇宙学设定下星形成率密度ρ_SFR的演化、成团性及其与物质场的相关性；重点考察ρ_SFR加权偏置量⟨bρ_SFR⟩和CIB-剪切交叉关联。

Result: 尽管多数按z=0星系恒星质量函数校准的模型预测相似，但当前⟨bρ_SFR⟩与CIB-剪切交叉关联观测明确排除强恒星反馈及强动能型（喷流驱动）AGN反馈模型。

Conclusion: CIB交叉关联是研究宇宙学背景下恒星与星系形成物理的重要新探针，可有效区分不同反馈模型。

Abstract: We study the evolution and clustering properties of the star formation rate density (SFR) $ρ_{\rm SFR}$ in the FLAMINGO suite of hydrodynamical simulations, with the aim of exploring the possibility of constraining AGN and stellar feedback prescription, as well as the effect of cosmology, using cross-correlations between the Cosmic Infrared Background (CIB) and other tracers of the large-scale structure. Specifically, we consider tomographic measurements of the bias-weighted SFR-density $\langle bρ_{\rm SFR}\rangle$, as well as cross-correlations between the CIB and cosmic shear as a probe of the $ρ_{\rm SFR}$-matter correlation. Most of the FLAMINGO models calibrated against the $z=0$ galaxy stellar mass function (SMF) yield similar predictions for the distribution and clustering of star-forming systems, and thus it is difficult to derive clean conclusions regarding the impact of AGN feedback or cosmology on CIB statistics. However, we show that current measurements of $\langle bρ_{\rm SFR}\rangle$, as well as CIB-shear cross-correlations clearly disfavour models with strong stellar feedback, as well as simulations with strong kinetic (jet-driven) AGN feedback, even under the fiducial SMF calibration. This study demonstrates the potential of CIB cross-correlations to study aspects of the physics of star and galaxy formation in a cosmological setting.

</details>


### [117] [Stochastic effect on ultralight dark matter searches with astronomical polarized sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08547)
*Kyohei Yamada*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的统计框架，用于分析超轻轴子类粒子（ALP）作为暗物质时引起的偏振角随机振荡信号，适用于任意尺寸和距离的光源，并划分了四种统计行为区域，同时强调了源距离不确定性对ALP-光子耦合约束的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻轴子类粒子（ALP）是具有良好理论动机的暗物质候选者，其在标准晕模型中表现为随机场，可能引起传播光偏振角的随机振荡；需建立普适统计框架以统一处理不同尺度天文与实验室探测中的该信号。

Method: 构建基于ALP场统计性质（如Compton长度、相干长度）的统一统计框架，按源尺寸与距离相对于这些长度的关系将信号划分为四类统计区域，并给出过渡区域的无缝描述；引入考虑信号随机性及源距离不确定性的似然函数。

Result: 成功复现了实验室、脉冲星及宇宙微波背景等已有探测场景的信号模型；定量表明源距离不确定性可使ALP-光子耦合约束改变达三倍；提供了跨尺度ALP偏振振荡探测的一致解释与比较基础。

Conclusion: 该框架为利用各类天文偏振源开展ALP搜索提供了严谨、统一且实用的统计工具，凸显了准确建模源参数不确定性的重要性。

Abstract: Ultralight axion-like particles (ALP) are the well-motivated candidates for the dark matter. In the standard halo model, the ultralight galactic dark matter behaves as a stochastic Gaussian random field, and the polarization angle of the linearly polarized light propagating through the field stochastically oscillates proportional to the field difference between the observer and the source. The statistics of this signal depend on the source size and its distance from the observer relative to the ALP Compton length and coherence length, and therefore on the ALP mass. We present a unified statistical framework for the polarization-oscillation searches with light sources of arbitrary size and distance. We classify the statistics of the stochastic oscillation amplitude into four distinct regimes and provide a seamless description including the transitional regimes between them. Our framework reproduces the signal models in previously known cases including the laboratory-scale searches and searches using pulsars and the cosmic microwave background. We provide a likelihood prescription that incorporates the stochasticity of the signal, and show that uncertainties in source distance must be carefully accounted for, as they can alter the resulting constraints on the ALP-photon coupling by up to a factor of three. Our results provide a consistent basis for interpreting and comparing a broad range of ALP searches with astronomical polarized sources.

</details>


### [118] [Boosted Odds: Lower-Point Probes of Parity Violation with Momentum Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08671)
*Yong Sheng Yap,William R. Coulton,Jiamin Hou,Steven Gratton*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用动量场及其密度-密度-动量三功率谱作为探测原初宇称破缺的新探针，相比传统四点统计方法更简洁高效，并在kSZ与星系巡天联合分析中展现出与现有方法相当的约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找暴胀动力学中单场慢滚范式之外的宇称破缺初值条件，因其是独特且易于与非线性引力及星系形成物理区分的信号。

Method: 引入动量场p=(1+δ)v，重点构建并分析密度-密度-动量三功率谱；考虑视线方向投影下的可观测性；开展针对超压扁模板宇称破缺振幅的Fisher预报，基于kSZ各向异性与星系聚类的交叉三功率谱。

Result: 该三功率谱对宇称破缺效应保持高灵敏度，即使仅能观测动量场沿视线方向的投影；Fisher预报显示其对宇称破缺振幅的约束能力与现有方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 密度-密度-动量三功率谱是一种新颖、低维、易实现的宇称破缺探针，可无缝融入现有巡天数据分析流程，并借助已有系统误差与协方差建模工作提升实际应用效果。

Abstract: Parity-violating initial conditions are uniquely compelling signatures of inflationary dynamics beyond the single-field slow-roll paradigm, and their effects on the distribution of late-time observables can be easily separated from that of gravitational nonlinearities and galaxy formation physics. In this work, we propose using the momentum field, $\mathbf{p} = (1+δ)\mathbf{v}$, and, in particular, the density-density-momentum bispectrum as a novel probe of primordial parity violation. This class of bispectra greatly complements traditional searches using four-point statistics of the density field, furnishing new observables that can be sensitive to parity-violating physics. At the same time, its lower dimensionality substantially reduces the complexity associated with analysing high-dimensional data vectors and their covariance. We show that the sensitivity of the density-density-momentum bispectrum to parity violation effects persists even if we can only observe the projection of the momentum field along the line-of-sight. We perform a Fisher forecast for the parity-violating amplitude of the ultra-squeezed template in the cross bispectrum of kinetic Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (kSZ) anisotropies and galaxy clustering, and we find that our approach give competitive constraints when compared to existing methods. Our method can be easily co-opted to existing pipelines to study parity violation and, as the galaxy-galaxy-kSZ bispectrum is a key cosmological probe, take advantage of the significant existing efforts dedicated to mitigating systematics and covariance modelling.

</details>


### [119] [When, Why, and How CMB Compression Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08728)
*Davide Pedrotti,William Giarè,Hanyu Cheng,Eleonora Di Valentino*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了压缩CMB似然函数在约束ΛCDM以外新物理时的适用性与失效条件，发现其在暗能量晚期演化等不改变暗物质背景演化的模型中仍可靠，但在暗物质演化被修改时会严重失真，导致后验分布几何结构发生多维畸变和参数约束显著偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定压缩CMB似然函数在扩展宇宙学模型中何时、为何及如何失效，以保障新物理参数限制的可靠性。

Method: 通过对比全Planck-CMB似然与在ΛCDM下校准的压缩CMB似然，在多种新物理模型（如晚期暗能量动力学、相互作用或衰变暗物质、非标准红移演化暗物质）中系统检验其约束一致性。

Result: 压缩似然在保持暗物质背景演化且无显著扰动效应的暗能量模型中仍准确；但一旦暗物质演化被修改，压缩似然会导致后验分布中心偏移、各向异性畸变、主模式重排序及本征方向旋转，边际化参数约束误差可达近一个数量级。

Conclusion: 压缩CMB似然并非普适工具，其适用性严格依赖于新物理是否扰动暗物质在CMB相关时期的演化历史；对暗物质晚期演化的修改会破坏压缩基础假设，导致不可接受的系统偏差。

Abstract: We test when compressed CMB likelihoods can be reliably used to constrain new physics beyond $Λ$CDM, formulating concrete criteria for \textit{when}, \textit{why}, and \textit{how} CMB compression fails. We derive our conclusions by exploring a broad set of models that modify different aspects of the cosmological evolution, including late-time dark-energy dynamics, non-standard late-time dark-matter evolution through interactions or decay, and dark-matter models with persistently non-standard redshift evolution. For each case, we compare the results obtained from data combinations based on the full Planck-CMB likelihood with their counterparts based on a compressed CMB likelihood calibrated in $Λ$CDM. We find that the compression remains accurate for late-time dark-energy extensions that preserve the dark-matter background evolution and do not introduce significant additional perturbative effects. By contrast, modifying the dark-matter evolution breaks the mapping between the present-day matter density and the matter density at the epochs relevant to the CMB, causing the compressed constraints to fail even when the modification acts exclusively at late times. In these cases, the compressed likelihood can drastically alter the geometry of the multidimensional posterior distributions, shifting their centers, producing strongly anisotropic deformations, reordering principal modes, and generating genuine mixing and rotation of their eigendirections. These multidimensional distortions propagate into substantially incorrect marginalized constraints on cosmological parameters, with differences between the full and compressed analyses reaching nearly an order of magnitude in some of the cases studied here.

</details>


### [120] [Halo Mass Function Constraints on Early Galaxy and AGN Populations in the JWST Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08731)
*Elan Yogeswaren,Robert Schaefer*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个将线性物质功率谱与暗物质晕质量函数（HMF）及可观测星系/类星体数量联系起来的理论框架，利用JWST在高红移发现的大质量星系和明亮类星体，对宇宙学结构形成与天体物理过程之间的联系进行定量检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST在高红shift观测到大量大质量星系和明亮活动星系核（AGN），为检验宇宙学结构形成模型与天体物理过程（如恒星形成、黑洞增长）之间的联系提供了日益灵敏的探针。

Method: 建立从线性物质功率谱到晕质量函数（HMF）、晕丰度及可观测星系/AGN数量的完整理论框架；以Sheth-Tormen（ST）HMF为基准，并对比Bolshoi-Klypin校准的KST模型，量化HMF校准不确定性对预测的影响；应用于三个互补检验：大质量星系的重子/恒星质量储备、可宿主明亮AGN的晕丰度、高红移紫外光度函数。

Result: 最致密系统位于晕分布的稀有高峰尾部，因此宿主晕质量、重子转化效率、黑洞占据率或占空比、以及HMF校准的微小变化，均会导致预测丰度显著变化；ST与KST比较表明，高红移晕丰度的不确定性会显著影响宿主晕供给推断（尤其在z~10）；单个源性质匹配不足以保证群体一致性，其对应宿主晕也必须在数量上充足。

Conclusion: 当前JWST观测正对宇宙学晕丰度及宇宙黎明时期的重子物理与黑洞物理构成日益严格的定量压力测试；需联合考虑晕统计与天体物理模型才能一致解释观测。

Abstract: The discovery by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) of massive galaxies and luminous active galactic nuclei (AGN) at high redshift provides an increasingly sensitive test of the connection between cosmological structure formation and the astrophysical processes that make dark matter halos observable. We develop a framework connecting the linear matter power spectrum to the mass variance, peak height, halo mass function (HMF), cumulative halo abundance, and observable galaxy and AGN populations. We use the Sheth-Tormen (ST) HMF as the baseline and compare it with an illustrative Bolshoi-Klypin-calibrated modification (KST) to quantify the sensitivity of predictions to the calibration of the HMF against cosmological N-body simulations. We apply the framework to three complementary tests: the baryonic and stellar mass reservoir available to massive galaxies, the abundance of halos capable of hosting luminous AGN, and the rest frame ultraviolet luminosity function of high redshift galaxies. Because the most massive systems occupy the rare, high peak tail of the halo distribution, modest changes in inferred host mass, baryonic conversion efficiency, black-hole occupation or duty cycle, and HMF calibration can produce large changes in predicted abundances. The ST-KST comparison shows that uncertainty in the high redshift halo population can materially affect the inferred host supply, particularly toward large z (10). Comparison with representative JWST massive galaxy, AGN, and UV luminosity function measurements demonstrates that matching the properties of an individual source is not sufficient for population consistency; the corresponding host halos must also exist in adequate numbers. Current observations provide increasingly stringent quantitative stress tests of both cosmological halo abundances and the baryonic and black hole physics at cosmic dawn.

</details>


### [121] [Mitigating prior-volume effects in galaxy clustering by adopting Mpc over Mpc/h units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08751)
*Andrea Pezzotta,Alejandro Pérez-Fernández,Giosuè Gambardella,Ariel G. Sánchez,Alexander Eggemeier,Martin Crocce,Benjamin Camacho-Quevedo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在基于EFT和VDG∞模型的星系聚类贝叶斯分析中先验体积效应的影响，发现该效应主要源于以Mpc/h为单位设定参数先验，导致与哈勃参数h的虚假相关；改用物理上更合理的Mpc单位可显著缓解该问题，并在多种宇宙学模型（如ΛCDM、w0waCDM等）下验证了其有效性，使参数约束偏差大幅减小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决星系聚类贝叶斯分析中因使用Mpc/h单位先验引发的先验体积效应及其对宇宙学参数估计的系统性偏移问题。

Method: 提出采用Mpc单位而非Mpc/h单位定义模型参数先验；在合成数据（模拟Stage-IV巡天信号）上测试该方法，并与不变先验、基于增长与几何振幅的重参数化（如用σ12替代σ8）等策略对比；加入BAO约束并检验低统计精度数据下的鲁棒性。

Result: 采用Mpc单位后，参数约束中由边缘化引起的偏移显著降低，尤其在w0waCDM模型中，与基准值的偏差从数个标准差减小至1σ内；σ12比σ8更优，且结果在加入BAO约束或降低统计精度时依然稳健。

Conclusion: 使用Mpc单位是缓解先验体积效应的有效、简洁且物理动机明确的方法，优于或等效于其他先进缓解策略，对Stage-IV星系巡天数据分析具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: We investigate prior-volume effects in Bayesian analyses of galaxy clustering based on state-of-the-art models, such as the EFT and the VDG$_\infty$ model. We show that these effects are largely driven by imposing priors on the model parameters defined in ${\rm Mpc}/h$ units, thereby introducing a spurious correlation with the Hubble parameter $h$, and that they can be strongly mitigated by adopting a more physically motivated formulation in ${\rm Mpc}$ units. We test this approach using a set of synthetic data vectors designed to reproduce the clustering signal expected in a Stage-IV survey across a range of cosmological models, including $Λ$CDM, $K$CDM, $w$CDM, and $w_0w_a$CDM. We find that the resulting constraints are largely free from shifts induced during the marginalisation process, even in the challenging $w_0w_a$CDM runs, reducing discrepancies with the fiducial values from several standard deviations to well within $1σ$. The performance of this approach is comparable to that of alternative mitigation strategies, such as the adoption of invariant priors or the reparametrisation of nuisance parameters in terms of growth and geometrical amplitudes. For the latter, we find that using $σ_{12}$ in place of the commonly adopted $σ_8$ to describe the amplitude of density fluctuations improves the performance of the reparametrisation technique, even at high redshifts, where constraints on the dark-energy equation of state are weaker. We further show that these conclusions hold with the inclusion of additional BAO constraints, and for data sets with reduced statistical power. These results add to the benefits of working with ${\rm Mpc}$ units, avoiding an explicit dependence on $h$, with direct relevance for the analyses of Stage-IV galaxy surveys.

</details>

### [122] [Mass as pairing: an explicit Majorana mass on half of a generation does not seesaw in symmetric mass generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07735)
*Piotr S. Topa*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种四维对称质量生成（SMG）模型，通过在一半Spin(10)类代中引入显式Majorana质量源，研究未被源作用的另一半费米子在无凝聚相中的行为；发现其单费米子响应极弱、配对敏感度微升，未观测到对称性破缺或轻质量通道显著增长，临界行为呈现饱和交叉而非幂律，且所有结论均附有可验证的证据类别与运行检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索对称质量生成（SMG）机制下，当一半费米子被显式质量源作用时，另一半在对称相中能否自然产生轻质量项或类跷跷板混合——这关系到SMG是否能替代传统希格斯或跷跷板机制解释费米子质量起源。

Method: 构建基于Spin(10)-类生成结构的四维格点SMG模型；引入色普适的Δ_R型显式质量源作用于右手中微子双态；系统测量单费米子读出、配对磁化率、σ通道响应等观测量，在体积4^4至8^4上进行格点模拟，并预注册检验协议（如MIXED方向测试）以避免后见偏差。

Result: （i）对称能隙相中：源被屏蔽至自由值1.1%以内，未源半的单费米子响应稳定在0.3%自由值，配对敏感度随源强h=2升至1.2–1.7%；无轻质量通道增长快于自由传播子，L≤8未见对称破缺迹象；（ii）临界点处：显式质量压垮σ临界通道，轻半趋向自由传播呈饱和交叉；MIXED方向测试支持临界耦合y_c随h上升，但y_c(h)未精确定位；未测量p→0时传播子极点/零点。另无一阶相变信号、发现Z_4-奇格点复合响应、正定性类具味普适性。

Conclusion: 在该SMG模型中，未被源作用的费米子半族无法自发产生可观测的轻质量项或跷跷板混合，除非存在对称性自发破缺；而当前参数范围内（L≤8），对称性保持精确（格点可证），故轻质量必须由源直接诱导或依赖尚未分辨的更大尺度物理；结果强化了SMG作为独立质量机制需满足严格对称约束的观点。

Abstract: A fermion mass pairs a field with a partner of conjugate charge: elementary (Higgs-Dirac), self-conjugate (seesaw) or a three-fermion composite (symmetric mass generation, SMG). We give half of a Spin(10)-like generation an explicit Majorana mass in a four-dimensional SMG model and ask what the other half does in a phase with no condensate. With the source on, every mass-type term of the unsourced half averages to zero under the symmetry group of the sourced action, so a light mass term, and a seesaw's light-heavy mixing, requires a spontaneously broken symmetry; exact on the lattice and a general proposition. The source is colour-democratic (a Delta_R-type mass on every flavour's right-handed taste doublet); the single-nu_R split lies outside the positivity class used but inside the symmetry statement. Measured up to 8^4: (i) in the symmetric-gap phase, with the source screened to at most 1.1% of free, the unsourced half's single-fermion readout stays at 0.3% of free while its pair susceptibility rises from 0.2% to 1.2-1.7% at h=2; on like mode sets it falls with the volume, on (4^4,8^4) no inequivalent light mass channel grows faster than free, and symmetry breaking is not resolved at L<=8; (ii) at the critical point the explicit mass collapses the critical sigma channel and moves the light half toward free as a saturating crossover, not a power law; direction tests (MIXED by their pre-registered letter) are consistent with a critical coupling rising with the mass; y_c(h) is not located. Pole versus zero of the light propagator at p->0 is not measured. The outcomes are readings after pre-registered tests that mostly returned no verdict. Also: no first-order signature of the merged transition at L<=8, a Z_4-odd response handed to a lattice composite, and flavour democracy of the positivity class. Every claim has an evidence class and a runnable check.

</details>

### [123] [All-Orders Classical Soft Graviton Theorems in the Newtonian Limit and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07134)
*Debanjan Karan,Babli Khatun,Raghu Mahajan,Biswajit Sahoo,Ashoke Sen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在牛顿极限下发现了一组无限阶的领头对数尾项，修正了经典软引力子定理，并指出Alessio等人提出的全阶猜想仅适用于两粒子散射，不适用于三粒子及以上情形；同时提出了超越牛顿极限的全阶猜想，虽未证明但通过多项非平凡自洽性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典软引力子定理仅能确定散射过程引力波初末态尾部的部分项，本文旨在拓展该定理，特别是在牛顿极限下探索更完整的尾项结构，并检验现有全阶猜想的适用范围。

Method: 基于渐近动量和低速入射/出射条件，在牛顿极限下系统计算引力波尾部的领头对数项；通过多粒子散射过程分析Alessio等人的全阶猜想的普适性；构造并检验超越牛顿极限的新型全阶猜想。

Result: 发现了牛顿极限下无限阶领头对数尾项；证实Alessio等人的全阶猜想仅对两粒子散射成立，三粒子及以上情形失效；提出并验证了一个新的、更普适的全阶猜想（尚未证明）。

Conclusion: 经典软引力子定理需在多粒子场景下进行实质性推广；牛顿极限下的领头对数结构具有丰富层次；新提出的全阶猜想为后续严格证明和高阶引力波建模提供了重要线索。

Abstract: The classical soft graviton theorem aims to determine certain terms in the early- and late-time tails of the gravitational waveform produced in a scattering process, using only the asymptotic momenta of the scattered objects. In this paper we find an infinite series of leading-logarithmic tail terms in the Newtonian limit, where the incoming and outgoing particles move slowly, but the scattering itself can be violent. The all-orders conjecture of Alessio, Di Vecchia and Heissenberg agrees with our results for two-particle scattering, but does not hold in general when the initial or final state contains three or more particles. We also propose an all-orders conjecture for these tails beyond the Newtonian limit, but do not prove it. Nevertheless, the conjecture passes several nontrivial consistency checks.

</details>


### [124] [A New Entropy Puzzle in ABJM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07135)
*Alexandre Belin,Seyed Morteza Hosseini,Palash Singh,Rita Vadala,Alberto Zaffaroni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出并解决了AdS$_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$中BPS黑洞熵随k增大而增加，与对偶ABJM理论中可贡献熵的规范不变算符数随k减小而增加之间的矛盾；提出trace relation数量随k减小而增加是主导机制，并以一个'偶然算符'在小k时消失为例予以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释BPS黑洞熵随k增大而增加，与对偶CFT中因单极子算符存在导致可贡献熵的规范不变算符数随k减小而增加之间的表观矛盾。

Method: 分析ABJM理论的超共形指标（superconformal index），考察有限N大荷极限下的行为，并提出基于trace relation数量变化的物理解释，辅以具体算符示例验证。

Result: 发现同一熵- k 矛盾在有限N大荷极限下依然存在；提出并论证随着k减小，trace relation数量急剧增加，压倒其他效应，从而统一解释熵行为；给出一个随k减小而消失的'偶然算符'实例。

Conclusion: 熵随k增加的本质原因并非可用算符数的单调变化，而是算符间代数约束（trace relations）随k减小而显著增强，导致有效独立自由度减少；该机制调和了引力侧与场论侧的熵计数。

Abstract: We point out a puzzle about BPS black holes in AdS$_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$: the entropy grows when $k$ increases although the number of gauge-invariant operators in the dual conformal field theory that can contribute to the entropy grows as $k$ decreases, due to the presence of monopole operators. Analyzing the superconformal index of ABJM, we show that the same puzzle exists for finite $N$ in the large charge limit. We propose a resolution based on an increasing number of trace relations as $k$ decreases, which dominates over the other effect. We argue that this mostly affects fortuitous operators and we provide an explicit example of a fortuitous operator that disappears at small $k$.

</details>


### [125] [Glueball gravitational form factors in dynamical holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08545)
*Kiminad A. Mamo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次通过全息方法研究了标量、赝标量和规范自旋平均张量胶球靶的引力形状因子（GFFs），提出了一种基于Einstein-标量-轴子模型的动态全息框架，并引入归一化重靶（HT）有效三次耦合以改善与格点数据的吻合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力形状因子可检验尚未通过谱和静态力校准确立物理有效性的引力三次耦合，需新的理论方法进行验证。

Method: 构建受N=4超杨-米尔斯理论变形启发的动态Einstein-标量-轴子全息模型；保留紫外源数据，采用类软墙红外重构；参数由谱和Wilson观测量在Sommer标度下共同固定；提出归一化重靶（HT）有效三次耦合方案。

Result: 原始三次顶点无法拟合27个标量格点观测值（χ²/dof=2.73），而HT方案在未调参情况下显著改善拟合（χ²/dof=0.63）；预测了赝标量与张量GFFs及三类靶的Breit压强/剪切分布；给出红外衰减轴子分支下的质量半径（0.276–0.311 fm）。

Conclusion: 引力三次耦合的匹配是独立于背景校准的关键步骤；HT方案为后续格点计算提供了可行的理论处方。

Abstract: We present the first holographic study of gravitational form factors (GFFs) of scalar, pseudoscalar and canonical-spin-averaged tensor glueball targets. These observables test gravitational cubic couplings whose physical validity is not established by spectra and static-force calibration. We construct a dynamical Einstein--scalar--axion representation inspired by the Babington--Crooks--Evans adjoint-mass deformation of $\mathcal N=4$ super-Yang--Mills theory and its Type-IIB supergravity dual. Leading ultraviolet source data are retained, while a soft-wall-like infrared reconstruction incorporates asymptotically linear radial mass-squared trajectories. Spectra and Wilson observables fix the parameters at a common Sommer scale without GFF input. The full constrained cubic vertices fail to describe 27 correlated scalar lattice observations, giving $χ^2/\mathrm{dof}=73.60/27=2.73$. We propose normalized heavy-target (HT) effective cubic couplings for pure-glue matching. HT adopts leading radial shapes suggested by infinitely heavy targets at fixed probe dynamics and normalizes their overlap weights on the physical ground states. With the background, modes and propagators unchanged, HT gives $χ^2/\mathrm{dof}=16.98/27=0.63$, without numerical adjustment to GFF data. Tensor and scalar source overlaps determine $A$ and the physical normalized trace; the exact finite-mass energy--momentum-tensor relation determines $D$. We predict the pseudoscalar and tensor form factors and three-target Breit pressure and shear profiles. On the reference infrared-decaying axion branch, the HT Breit mass radii are $0.276$, $0.300$ and $0.311\,\mathrm{fm}$, respectively. The results identify gravitational cubic matching as a distinct step beyond background calibration and provide a prescription for further lattice tests.

</details>


### [126] [Checking the Schwinger-Dyson equation in the purely open matrix-tensor model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07141)
*Daniel L. Jafferis,Liza Rozenberg,Diandian Wang,Cynthia Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Schwinger-Dyson关系检验三维引力的矩阵-张量描述，重点分析纯开弦扇区中的圆柱振幅，并依据Virasoro TQFT启发的计数规则对拓扑贡献进行分类与计数，验证了两种相互作用项对应流形数目的单位差猜想，强调了引力路径积分中明确指定体拓扑集合的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验矩阵-张量框架下三维引力的有效性，并厘清引力路径积分中应包含哪些体拓扑结构。

Method: 利用Schwinger-Dyson关系分析纯开弦扇区的圆柱振幅；对拓扑贡献进行枚举与分类；采用Virasoro TQFT启发的计数规则对比不同嵌入方式下的流形计数。

Result: 在所提计数规则下，两种相互作用项对应的流形数目之差为1，与猜想一致；而若单独计数所有不等价曲面嵌入，则结果不匹配。

Conclusion: 拓扑计数规则的选择显著影响结果，凸显在引力路径积分中明确定义所含体拓扑集合的必要性。

Abstract: We test the proposed matrix-tensor description of three-dimensional gravity using a Schwinger-Dyson relation in its purely open sector. We enumerate and classify the topological contributions to a cylinder amplitude. Under a proposed counting prescription motivated by Virasoro TQFT, we find agreement with the conjectured unit difference between the numbers of contributing manifolds associated to the two interaction terms in the potential. By contrast, counting all inequivalent surface embeddings separately would instead give a mismatch. This comparison highlights the importance of specifying the set of bulk topologies to be included in the gravitational path integral.

</details>


### [127] [The Back-to-Back Plateau of the Energy-Energy Correlator from Weak to Strong Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07145)
*Hao Chen,Ian Moult,Bo Wang,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了能量-能量关联器（EEC）在背对背极限下的有限平台行为，通过N³LL精度下对领头和次领头幂次对数的重求和，并结合bootstrap与强耦合数据，构建了从弱耦合到强耦合的平台高度插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解EEC在背对背极限下的平台行为，连接弱耦合下的软-共线辐射与强耦合下的弦动力学。

Method: 在N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中，通过N³LL精度重求和领头和次领头幂次对数，并引入人类参与的智能体工作流；结合bootstrap方法和强耦合数据进行插值构造。

Result: 得到了EEC背对背极限平台高度从弱耦合到强耦合的可靠插值结果，并包含了非平面贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作成功桥接了弱耦合微扰计算与强耦合非微扰物理，展示了多方法融合与人机协同在高能理论计算中的有效性。

Abstract: The back-to-back limit of the energy-energy correlator (EEC) exhibits a finite plateau, capturing the evolution from perturbative soft-collinear radiation at weak coupling to string dynamics at strong coupling. At weak coupling, we resum its leading and next-to-leading-power logarithms through N$^3$LL in $\mathcal N=4$ SYM, including non-planar contributions. Combining this weak-coupling endpoint with bootstrap and strong-coupling data, we construct an interpolation for the plateau height from weak to strong coupling. The calculation uses a human-in-the-loop agentic workflow.

</details>


### [128] [Deriving the $\text{AdS}_3\times\text{S}^3\times \text{T}^4$ Quantum Spectral Curve II: T-system and QSC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07147)
*Andrea Cavaglià,Davide Polvara,Nicolò Primi,Alessandro Sfondrini,Roberto Tateo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文完成了AdS3×S3×T4纯RR通量弦论中量子谱曲线（QSC）的系统推导，建立了热力学Bethe假设与QSC之间的精确关系，推导出先前猜想的QSC，并揭示了其解所满足的额外关系；同时阐明了质量为零激发如何通过QSC函数的解析结构隐式编码，最终得到紧凑的Pμ-系统表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完成AdS3×S3×T4纯RR通量弦论中量子谱曲线（QSC）的系统性推导，并厘清其与热力学Bethe Ansatz及质量零激发的关系。

Method: 基于前文所得扩展Y-系统，系统推导QSC；建立TBA与QSC形式主义的精确对应；分析QSC解的解析性质以揭示隐含的质量零激发信息；最终导出紧凑的Pμ-系统。

Result: 成功推导出AdS3×S3×T4纯RR背景下的QSC；确立了TBA与QSC的严格联系；发现QSC解满足的额外约束关系；阐明质量零激发由QSC函数的解析结构隐式编码；获得简洁的Pμ-系统表述。

Conclusion: QSC可系统、自洽地描述该背景下的全谱问题，其解析结构天然容纳质量零模，Pμ-系统为实际计算提供了高效框架。

Abstract: We complete the systematic derivation of the Quantum Spectral Curve (QSC) for strings on $\text{AdS}_3\times\text{S}^3\times \text{T}^4$ with pure Ramond--Ramond (RR) flux, building on the extended Y-system obtained in our preceding paper. We establish the precise relation between the two formalisms of the Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz and the QSC, derive the previously conjectured QSC, and, along the way, uncover additional relations satisfied by its solutions. Our analysis also clarifies how massless excitations, although not explicit among the basic QSC building blocks, are encoded in their analytic structure. The derivation culminates in the so-called $\mathbf{P}μ$-system, a compact formulation of the QSC in terms of a small set of functions and their analytic continuations.

</details>


### [129] [Localizing $\mathcal{N}=(2,0)$ $AdS_3$ solutions of M-theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07153)
*Carolina Matté Gregory,Catherine Notman,James Sparks*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文应用等变定位法研究十一维超引力中AdS3×M8背景下的二维N=(2,0) SCFT对偶，将保护观测量约化为U(1) R-对称性作用的不动点数据，并导出中心荷与引力反常的拓扑公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解二维N=(2,0)超共形场论及其在AdS/CFT对应中的引力实现，需系统计算受超对称保护的物理量（如中心荷、引力反常），而传统方法在复杂几何背景下难以实施。

Method: 采用等变定位技术处理AdS3×M8型超引力解；构造等变闭多形式以编码磁通、校准M2膜及翘曲体积；聚焦于S^4丛与S^2丛两类几何构型，结合上同调与拓扑量子化条件进行分析。

Result: 得到了中心荷和引力反常的显式拓扑公式，仅依赖于量子化通量与底流形拓扑不变量；复现了T^2×KE_4情形的已知结果，并构造出一个不在已知多项式分支内的新紧致数值解。

Conclusion: 等变定位为计算受超对称保护的可观测量提供了强大且普适的几何工具，揭示了通量量子化与共形场论反常之间的深层拓扑联系，并拓展了AdS3/CFT2对应中可解背景的范围。

Abstract: We apply equivariant localization to supersymmetric $AdS_3\times M_8$ solutions of eleven-dimensional supergravity, dual to two-dimensional $\mathcal N=(2,0)$ SCFTs. We construct equivariantly closed polyforms encoding the magnetic flux and its dual, calibrated M2-branes and the warped volume, reducing protected observables to fixed point data of the $U(1)$ R-symmetry action. We apply the framework to $S^4$ bundles over coassociative four-cycles and $S^2$ bundles over compact six-manifolds, obtaining topological formulae for leading central charges and gravitational anomalies in terms of quantized fluxes and topological data. We recover the known results for $T^2\times KE_4$ solutions and construct a compact numerical solution with quantized flux that lies outside the known polynomial branch.

</details>


### [130] [The s-Rule from Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07154)
*Sebastian Franco,Diego Rodriguez-Gomez*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解五维量子场论中几何工程与五膜网两种描述在存在七膜时的关系，需将s-规则纳入几何框架；特别是需阐明s-规则在七膜形变中的几何含义。

Method: 聚焦于终止于七膜的基本三叉五膜网，基于五膜终止于七膜的局域M理论描述，研究含单条非本原边的三角形环面图；分析其对应的复结构形变在相邻环面片重叠区域的定义性及胶合映射的良定义性。

Result: 发现复结构形变在重叠区域良好定义，且胶合映射在整个邻近环面片上保持良行为当且仅当满足s-规则；s-规则饱和时几何为T-锥，七膜形变为其Q-Gorenstein光滑化。

Conclusion: s-规则本质上是局部七膜形变无法全局化的 obstruction；该工作首次给出了T-锥与五膜终止于七膜之间明确的几何对应关系。

Abstract: String Theory provides powerful tools for constructing and studying five-dimensional Quantum Field Theories, notably through geometric engineering and five-brane webs. Understanding how these two descriptions are related in the presence of 7-branes requires incorporating the s-rule into the geometry. To address this question, we focus on basic three-legged five-brane webs ending on 7-branes. Building on the local M-theory description of five-branes ending on 7-branes, we consider triangular toric diagrams with a single non-primitive edge. We show that the corresponding local complex-structure deformation is well defined on the overlaps with the neighboring toric patches, while the corresponding gluing map remains well behaved as a function of the holomorphic coordinates throughout the neighboring patches precisely when the s-rule is satisfied. At saturation, the geometry is a T-cone and the 7-brane deformation becomes its Q-Gorenstein smoothing. Our results thus identify the s-rule with the obstruction to globalizing the local 7-brane deformation and provide a direct geometric link between T-cones and five-branes ending on 7-branes.

</details>
