Result: 发现该类磁体可呈现混合铁磁-反铁磁行为；获得了双原子SU(2)分子磁体的完整相结构与相变特性。

Conclusion: SU(N)-不变分子磁体展现出超越传统磁体的丰富相行为，平均场理论成功刻画了其混合磁序及相变规律，为多分量强关联磁性系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study magnets composed of molecules with atoms interacting via $SU(N)$-invariant two-body interactions. Atoms carry states transforming in generically different irreducible representations of $SU(N)$, and the interaction strength between atoms depends on the specific atoms involved. We derive the thermodynamics of these magnets in the mean-field approach, and find that they can exhibit mixed ferro-antiferromagnetic behavior. We present a full analysis of the simplest nontrivial case of molecules consisting of two atoms in the spin-$j$ representation of $SU(2)$, which generalizes conventional magnetic systems, and derive its phase structure and transitions.

</details>


### [132] [Non-invertible SPT phases from fusion rings via Tannaka duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07165)
*Antonio Santaniello,Roberto Valandro,Massimo Zorzenon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有非可逆对称性的(1+1)维量子场论中，如何仅从融合环出发提取纤维函子，以判断对称性是否无反常，并应用于Tambara-Yamagami环及两个27阶特殊群的表示范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合规则是研究非可逆对称性最易获取的数据，但不足以判断对称性是否无反常（即是否存在平凡能隙相）；而平凡能隙相由对应融合范畴的纤维函子刻画。

Method: 基于Tannaka对偶性，通过同时求解幺半结构映射和张量自同构，仅从融合环中提取纤维函子，避免直接求解pentagon方程。

Result: 成功应用于Z2×Z2的Tambara-Yamagami环；对两个27阶特殊群共享的秩为11的融合环，发现多个纤维函子（即多个非可逆SPT相），并据此导出其F-符号的简洁表达式及不同SPT相界面处的反常代数。

Conclusion: 仅凭融合环即可系统寻找纤维函子，从而判定非可逆对称性的反常性质，并构造相应的SPT相与界面物理。

Abstract: In a (1+1)-dimensional QFT with non-invertible symmetry, the fusion rules of topological lines are often the most accessible data, yet they do not fully determine whether the symmetry is anomaly-free, i.e. whether it admits trivially gapped phases. Such phases are characterized by fiber functors of the corresponding fusion category. Building on Tannaka duality, we extract fiber functors from the fusion ring alone by solving simultaneously for monoidal structure maps and tensor automorphisms. This allows us to find categorifications admitting a fiber functor without solving the pentagon equations directly. We test this procedure on the known Tambara--Yamagami ring of $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$. Our main result concerns the rank-11 fusion ring shared by the representation categories of the two extraspecial groups of order 27, which has a multiplicity 3 channel and is relevant to self-triality. In both cases we find more than one fiber functor, i.e. several non-invertible SPT phases. We use the explicit data of these fiber functors to derive the $F$-symbols of the two representation categories in a compact form depending on a non-degenerate alternating bicharacter and an invertible object $ε$. Using the reconstructed data, we compute the anomalous algebra on the interface between two different SPT phases.

</details>


### [133] [Multi-Point Affine Bialgebras for AdS/CFT Integrability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07170)
*Niklas Beisert,Egor Im,Hannes Malcha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了AdS/CFT世界面散射和一维Hubbard模型中可积结构所依赖的经典仿射李双代数的解析结构，通过引入具有多个奇点的亚纯函数（而非传统的洛朗级数展开），严格刻画其奇点，并构建了适用于有理与三角情形的三、四点仿射代数，最终基于Krichever-Novikov代数构造出一致的拟三角李双代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期工作发现该仿射李双代数在参数平面上除通常的0和∞外还存在两个额外奇点，但仅用洛朗展开难以严格处理这些奇点；本文旨在用亚纯函数框架严谨分析并重构其代数结构。

Method: 细致分析复平面上仿射双代数关系的解析结构，尝试以额外奇点为洛朗展开中心进行重写，继而提出三、四点仿射代数，并借助Krichever-Novikov代数构造多 puncture 黎曼球面上的合适亚纯函数基，验证其构成拟三角李双代数。

Result: 成功构建了适用于有理与三角情形的三、四点仿射代数；证明了在Krichever-Novikov函数基下，这些多点代数构成一致的拟三角李双代数。

Conclusion: 通过引入多点解析结构与Krichever-Novikov代数框架，本文超越了传统单点洛朗展开的局限，为AdS/CFT与Hubbard模型中的可积结构提供了更普适、严格的代数基础。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the analytical structure of the classical affine Lie bialgebra underlying the integrable structures of AdS/CFT worldsheet scattering and the one-dimensional Hubbard model. Earlier work showed that the algebraic relations implied two singularity locations in the evaluation parameter plane besides the common expansion points infinity and zero for Laurent polynomials forming the basis for loop and affine algebras. Our aim is to formulate these singularities rigorously using meromorphic functions rather than by expansion into geometric series.
  We thus carefully analyse the analytical structure of the affine bialgebra relations in the complex plane paying attention to potential restrictions and extensions due to the additional singularities. As a first attempt with some shortcomings, we reformulate the affine bialgebra relations with the extra singularities serving as the expansion points for Laurent polynomials. We finally overcome the remaining issues by proposing three-point and four-point affine algebras for the rational and trigonometric cases. With a suitable choice of basis for meromorphic functions on the Riemann sphere with several punctures based on Krichever-Novikov algebras, we show that the multi-point affine algebras form consistent quasi-triangular Lie bialgebras.

</details>


### [134] [Generalised entanglement wedges in three dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07176)
*Abhisek Sahu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究广义纠缠楔形（GWs）在AdS₃真空中的几何与信息论起源，证明其单调性和强子可加性等性质直接源于边界CFT中区间冯·诺依曼熵的强子可加性，并指出GWs可能对应比边界子区域更一般的CFT子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究广义纠缠楔形（GWs）的性质（如嵌套性、单调性、强子可加性）是否像普通纠缠楔形一样，有其对偶CFT中的信息论根源。

Method: 在AdS₃真空、领头阶G下，首先证明静态切片上每个GW是测地凸区域的并；每个凸区域是若干普通纠缠楔形的交；再利用积分几何中的Crofton公式将凸区域周长表示为与其相交的测地线测度；结合AdS₃中每条测地线对应一个边界区间的RT面，将该测度表达为CFT区间熵的组合；最后通过测地线集合间的简单不等式导出GW性质。

Result: 证明了GW的单调性和强子可加性可直接由边界CFT熵的强子可加性推出；GW的周长测度恒正；GW可表为普通纠缠楔形的并和交；暗示GW可能对应CFT中比边界子区域更一般的子系统。

Conclusion: GWs的量子信息性质并非仅来自引力几何，而是根植于边界CFT的熵不等式；这强化了全息原理中边界信息决定体物理的观点，并拓展了CFT中‘子系统’的概念。

Abstract: Generalized entanglement wedges (GWs), proposed by Bousso and Penington for gravitating regions in general spacetimes, share many properties of ordinary entanglement wedges, including nesting, monotonicity and strong subadditivity of their generalized entropies. In AdS/CFT, the corresponding properties of entanglement wedges are consequences of the structure of the dual CFT. In this paper we ask whether the properties of GWs have a similar origin. We explore this question in vacuum AdS$_3$ at leading order in $G$. We first show that every GW on a static slice is a union of geodesically convex regions. Each such region is an intersection of ordinary entanglement wedges, so every GW is built from unions and intersections of entanglement wedges. We then use the Crofton formula of integral geometry to write the perimeter of a convex region as the measure of the set of geodesics that intersect it. In AdS$_3$ every geodesic is the Ryu--Takayanagi surface of a boundary interval, so this measure can be written in terms of the von Neumann entropies of CFT intervals. Strong subadditivity of the CFT entropy guarantees that the measure is positive. Monotonicity and strong subadditivity of GWs then follow from simple inequalities between sets of geodesics. The strong subadditivity of GWs is therefore a direct consequence of strong subadditivity on the boundary. Our results further suggest that GWs correspond to subsystems of the CFT that are more general than boundary subregions.

</details>


### [135] [Geometry of massive loops in gravitational scattering: Maximal cut, Riemann's bilinear relations and genus drop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07234)
*Arpan Bhattacharyya,Saptaswa Ghosh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有质量中介子的经典引力散射中一个典型的两圈世界线量子场论积分族，发现其最大割在质量非零时呈现椭圆结构，在质量为零或静态极限下退化为节点曲线；通过Baikov表示和积分约化，揭示了该积分的椭圆与多对数主积分分解、微分方程结构、周期矩阵表达式、单值性关系及椭圆多重多对数函数空间等五方面核心结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解经典引力散射中质量中介子导致的量子修正，特别是两圈精度下超越多对数的椭圆结构起源及其几何与代数性质。

Method: 采用世界线QFT框架、最大割分析、Baikov表示、积分-by-parts（IBP）约化、Laporta算法、交截理论、椭圆曲线周期计算、Appell与Beta函数、扭曲Riemann周期关系、单值性分析及Bessel变换等综合方法。

Result: 1) 两圈最大割为椭圆曲线周期；2) 得到5个主积分，分为3维椭圆块与2维多对数块；3) 给出任意维度下周期矩阵的Appell/Beta函数表达及9个二次恒等式；4) 建立满足Riemann双线性关系的微分系统的全局单值关系；5) 明确椭圆多重多对数函数空间，并揭示动量空间中Bessel扭曲微分的额外对称性与周期商空间分类。

Conclusion: 质量引入的椭圆性是两圈经典引力散射的关键新特征，其几何与代数结构已系统刻画，为完整求解含质量两圈积分奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We study a representative two-loop family of worldline quantum field theory integrals relevant for classical gravitational scattering with a massive mediator and investigate the geometric structure of its maximal cut. We find that, unlike the massless case, the massive loops already give rise to an elliptic maximal cut at two loops, which degenerates into a nodal curve in the massless limit and in the static limit. In the global Baikov representation, two successive integrations reduce the maximal cut to a period of an elliptic curve. We further establish five primary consequences of this study: (a) The integration-by-parts method generates five master integrals, which, under reflection, split into a three-dimensional elliptic block and a two-dimensional polylog block. We derive the differential equation in two ways, one by Laporta reduction and subsequently by intersection theory in two cocycle bases, and construct the rational map between them. (b) The period matrices in arbitrary dimension have been computed in terms of Appell and Beta functions. Furthermore, we derive twisted Riemann period relations that yield nine quadratic identities among them. (c) We study the global monodromy relation of the differential system that satisfies Riemann's bilinear relation. (d) We explicitly derive the function space of the iterated integrals in terms of elliptic multiple polylogs. (e) In impact-parameter space, the Fourier-transformed cut is a Bessel-twisted differential on the elliptic curve, which we show has extra involution symmetries. Thus, the parity of the differential further decides which quotient - rational or elliptic its periods live on. All results presented concern the maximal cut and, therefore, the homogeneous solutions, but they open an important pathway to tackling the full massive two-loop integral.

</details>


### [136] [The QCD phase diagram and critical end point at finite magnetic field from holography with neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07353)
*Shaojie Liu,Yehong Zhang,Hui Liu,Xuanmin Cao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用五维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量场（EMD）模型，结合神经网络校准的全息径向演化方法，研究了零重子化学势下强相互作用物质在磁场驱动下的QCD热相变临界端点（CEP），发现随磁场增强，相变由交叉变为一级，并确定了CEP位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强相互作用物质在强磁场下的相结构，特别是QCD热相变的临界端点，以理解极端条件下的夸克胶子等离子体性质，并与格点QCD结果进行对比验证。

Method: 采用五维Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton（EMD）全息模型，通过神经网络拟合全息径向演化来校准模型参数，使其符合零场和有限磁场下的格点熵数据；利用温度拐点合并、比热发散、声速平方为零等判据定位临界端点；用s/T^3的拐点定义交叉温度，用Maxwell等压构造确定一级共存线。

Result: 确定磁场驱动的临界端点位于(eB_c, T_c) ≈ (7.22 GeV², 95.9 MeV)；相变随磁场增强由交叉转变为一级，与格点QCD定性一致；给出了该模型校准后的相边界预测。

Conclusion: 全息EMD模型经神经网络校准后能合理描述磁场对QCD热相变的影响，成功再现临界端点及相变阶数变化，为强磁场下QCD相图提供了可检验的理论预言。

Abstract: We study the magnetic-field-driven critical end point (CEP) of the QCD thermal transition at zero baryon chemical potential using a five-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton (EMD) model. We calibrate the model to lattice entropy at zero and finite magnetic fields using a neural-network formulation of holographic radial evolution. As the magnetic field increases, the thermal transition changes from a crossover to a first-order transition. The merger of temperature turning points, a divergent specific heat, and a vanishing squared sound speed identify $(eB_c,T_c)\simeq(7.22\,\mathrm{GeV}^{2},95.9\,\mathrm{MeV})$. The transition-region inflection of $s/T^3$ defines the crossover temperature, while an equal-pressure Maxwell construction determines the first-order coexistence line. The two lines meet at the CEP. The change of transition order agrees qualitatively with lattice results. The phase boundary is a prediction of the calibrated model.

</details>


### [137] [Quantum Algorithms for Finding Vacua in the ADK Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07439)
*Shirabe Endo,Yuta Hamada*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Arkani-Hamed、Dimopoulos和Kachru提出的弦景观玩具模型中，利用量子算法寻找具有小宇宙学常数真空态的问题。作者首先构建了一个函数值预言机并用于Grover搜索，查询复杂度为O(2^{N/2})；随后利用真空能量的线性性质将问题转化为比特串两块之间的碰撞问题，并设计出两种查询复杂度为O(2^{N/3})的量子算法，显著优于Denef和Douglas的经典伪多项式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弦理论的景观模型中，寻找具有极小宇宙学常数的真空态是关键且计算困难的问题，经典算法效率低，亟需更高效的量子算法。

Method: 构建函数值预言机并应用Grover搜索；进一步利用真空能量的线性结构，将问题转化为比特串分块碰撞问题，并据此设计两种O(2^{N/3})查询复杂度的量子算法。

Result: 提出了两种查询复杂度为O(2^{N/3})的量子算法，相较经典伪多项式算法（Denef-Douglas）在物理相关参数下大幅降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 利用问题内在结构设计的量子算法可在弦景观真空搜索中实现超越平方根加速，凸显量子计算在基础物理问题中的潜在优势。

Abstract: We study quantum algorithms for finding a vacuum with a small cosmological constant in the toy model for string Landscape proposed by Arkani-Hamed, Dimopoulos, and Kachru. First, we construct a function-value oracle and use it for Grover search with query complexity $\mathcal{O}(2^{N/2})$, where $N$ is the number of scalar fields. Then, we point out that, by exploiting the linearity of the vacuum energy, the problem can be reduced to a collision problem between two blocks of the bit string. With this structure, we construct two quantum algorithms with query complexity $\mathcal{O}(2^{N/3})$. Compared with the pseudo-polynomial classical algorithm of Denef and Douglas, the quantum algorithms yield a large reduction in computational cost for the physically motivated parameter values.

</details>


### [138] [Soft-photon theorem near static spherical horizons in generic dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07498)
*Kai-Yu Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维静态球对称时空中的麦克斯韦理论在非极端分叉Killing视界附近的性质，构建了视界相空间与大U(1)电荷，并推导出基于大U(1) Ward恒等式的软光子关系，与平直空间软光子定理进行了对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞视界附近电磁场的自由度结构及软光子物理，尤其在高维和非极端情形下如何推广软定理与电荷守恒。

Method: 在正则Eddington-Finkelstein坐标系中施加近视界边界条件，构造视界相空间与大U(1)电荷；通过Killing频率与球谐展开分析视界场，结合大U(1) Ward恒等式导出软光子关系；并与平直空间软光子定理在相同球谐基和对易归一化下比较。

Result: 发现D=4时视界软光子关系与平直空间软定理在软频依赖和硬电荷角依赖上完全一致；而D>4时平直空间结果出现额外的维数依赖的频与角因子；且非极端有限半径视界支持整数幂辐射展开，区别于奇数维无穷远的半整数幂衰减。

Conclusion: 软光子关系在四维具有普适性，可由视界对称性自然导出；高维情形揭示了软定理对时空维度的敏感性，提示需重新审视大规范对称性在弯曲时空中的实现方式。

Abstract: We study Maxwell theory near a nonextremal bifurcate Killing horizon in a general static, spherically symmetric spacetime of dimension $D>3$. We impose near-horizon boundary conditions in a regular Eddington--Finkelstein chart that retain both radiative and Coulombic data, and construct the associated horizon phase space and large-$U(1)$ charge. At null infinity, the radiative falloff depends on the spacetime dimension and involves half-integer powers in odd $D$, whereas a nonextremal horizon at finite radius supports radiative data with an ordinary integer-power expansion. Expanding the horizon fields in Killing frequency and spherical harmonics, we derive the associated soft-photon relation from the large-$U(1)$ Ward identity. For comparison, we express the flat-space leading soft-photon theorem using the same spherical harmonics and commutator normalization as the horizon modes. In $D=4$, the two relations have the same soft-frequency dependence and the same angular dependence of the hard-charge terms, whereas for $D>4$ the flat-space relation contains additional dimension-dependent frequency and angular factors.

</details>


### [139] [Spin-1 Bulk Local States in AdS/CFT and Carrollian Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07548)

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了AdS₃/CFT₂和三维Carrollian全息中质量自旋-1场的体局域态，并证明其在两种框架下均分裂为两个确定螺旋度分支，支持诱导表示作为Carrollian全息中质量体激发的自然框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立具有自旋的质量体局域态，统一AdS/CFT与Carrollian全息描述，并验证二者在平直极限下的连续性。

Method: 利用保持体点不变的等距对称性确定AdS₃中质量矢量场的三个分量；通过HKLL重构及其边界后裔展开验证态结构；在Carrollian诱导表示中求解相同约束，并分析AdS到Carrollian的平直极限。

Result: 成功构造出完整的质量自旋-1体局域态，包括态本身、体-体传播子，并揭示AdS与Carrollian描述间通过极限的一致对应；两个螺旋度分支分别对应Proca场（宇称不变）和Maxwell–Chern–Simons单模。

Conclusion: 诱导表示是Carrollian全息中质量体激发的恰当框架；这是首个从态、传播子到极限关系均完备的带自旋体局域态构造。

Abstract: We construct the bulk local states of a massive spin-1 field in AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ and three-dimensional Carrollian holography. In AdS, covariance under the isometries that leave a bulk point invariant determines the three components of the vector there and splits the solution into two branches of definite helicity, whose inner products reproduce the massive vector two-point function of AdS$_3$. By expanding the massive vector HKLL representation in boundary descendants, one can reproduce the three component states term by term. The two branches together describe the parity-invariant Proca field, and one alone the single propagating mode of Maxwell--Chern--Simons theory. The same conditions are solved in the induced representation of the Carrollian theory, where the solution again splits into two branches. Under the flat limit of the generators the highest weight module becomes the induced module, and the AdS states and Green's functions become their Carrollian counterparts, which supports the induced representation as the home of massive bulk excitations in Carrollian holography. This is the first bulk local state built with spin, complete from the state itself to its bulk-to-bulk propagator and to the limit relating the two descriptions.

</details>


### [140] [Thermodynamics and mechanical laws of causal-diamond horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07746)
*Carlos R. Ordóñez,Gustavo Valdivia-Mera*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了闵可夫斯基时空因果钻石视界的统一热力学与力学框架，推导出温度、熵-面积关系及局域热力学第一、第二和第零定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为闵可夫斯基时空中的因果钻石视界建立一个自洽的热力学与力学描述框架，揭示其作为时空因果结构基本单元所具有的热力学性质。

Method: 基于欧氏正则性确定温度；通过截断的Gibbons-Hawking-York作用量计算欧氏作用量；结合Raychaudhuri方程与Killing能量流分析微弱物质扰动下的面积响应；利用共形表面引力论证第零律。

Result: 得到因果钻石视界温度T_D=1/(πα)、熵S_D=A_0/(4G)，并确立局域物理过程第一定律、满足零能条件时的局域第二定律，以及由均匀共形表面引力保证的第零律。

Conclusion: 因果钻石虽非真实黑洞视界，但仍可展现出完整的热力学定律结构，表明热力学性质可源于纯几何与因果结构，而无需引力动力学或弯曲时空背景。

Abstract: We develop a unified thermodynamic and mechanical framework for causal-diamond horizons in Minkowski spacetime. Euclidean regularity fixes the temperature $T_D=1/(πα)$, where $2α$ is the temporal extent of the diamond. The regulated Gibbons--Hawking--York contribution gives $I_D^E=-A_0/(4G)$, with $A_0$ the area of the horizon bifurcation surface, and the vanishing equilibrium geometric energy then yields the entropy--area relation $S_D=A_0/(4G)$. For weak matter perturbations crossing a localized portion of the future horizon, a Raychaudhuri analysis relates the associated Killing-energy flux to the area response, yielding a local physical-process first law at leading order near the bifurcation surface and, for classical matter satisfying the null energy condition, a local second law. Uniform conformal surface gravity establishes the zeroth law, while zero temperature is approached only in the limit $α\to\infty$, in which the finite causal horizon disappears. These results show that a coherent horizon-law structure can arise within a causal diamond, a fundamental building block of the causal structure of Minkowski spacetime. The derivation is explicit in $(2+1)$ dimensions, while the physical results retain the same form for spherical causal diamonds in $d\geq3$.

</details>


### [141] [Spontaneous Hawking Radiation from Dynamical Black-Hole Formation in Quantum Spin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07794)
*Shunichiro Kinoshita,Keiju Murata,Daisuke Yamamoto,Ryosuke Yoshii*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在局域量子自旋模型中实现动态黑洞形成所引发的霍金辐射的方法，通过模拟时空几何演化，成功将连续场论中的霍金粒子产生与自旋可观测量联系起来，并定量复现了包括表面引力依赖性的能量密度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在实验可及的多体系统中模拟弯曲时空中的量子场论（特别是霍金辐射），建立微观可实现的量子模拟路径。

Method: 从对应早期真空的基态出发，模拟动态黑洞形成所引起的几何演化；在连续量子场论中分析in-与out-模之间的非平凡Bogoliubov混合，导出重整化局域能量密度；将其映射为自旋链上的局域自旋可观测量。

Result: 识别出一个局域自旋可观测量，其期望值在有限时间窗口内（时长随系统尺寸对数增长）精确复现连续场论预言的霍金谱加权能量密度，包含对表面引力的正确依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了弯曲时空量子场论的微观量子模拟方案，首次将场论中的粒子产生机制直接关联到实验可测的多体可观测量，揭示了普适连续霍金物理向晶格截断动力学的 crossover 行为。

Abstract: We show that Hawking radiation spontaneously generated by dynamical black-hole formation can be realized in a local quantum spin model. Starting from the ground state corresponding to the early-time vacuum, with no initially prepared excitations, we follow the dynamics induced by the evolving geometry. In the corresponding continuum quantum field theory, Hawking particle creation is associated with nontrivial Bogoliubov mixing between the in- and out-mode decompositions. We derive the resulting renormalized local energy density in the continuum quantum field theory, which corresponds to an energy-weighted integral of the Hawking spectrum, and identify its counterpart as a local spin observable whose expectation value quantitatively reproduces the continuum prediction, including its dependence on the surface gravity. The agreement persists over a finite time window whose duration grows logarithmically with system size, revealing the crossover from universal continuum Hawking physics to lattice-cutoff dynamics. These results establish a microscopic route to quantum simulation of quantum field theory in curved spacetime, connecting field-theoretic particle creation directly to experimentally accessible many-body observables.

</details>


### [142] [Reheating surface volume renormalization and eternal inflation under broken scale invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07807)
*Min-Seok Seo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了尺度不变性破缺对永恒暴胀条件的影响，发现当谱指数偏离1（即ε<0）时，再加热面体积的分形结构在宏观尺度上瓦解，从而缓解了‘无永恒暴胀’条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测表明尺度不变性存在微小破缺（由谱指数ns偏离1表征），而此前工作指出尺度不变性下永恒暴胀源于再加热面体积重整化失败；本文旨在探究该破缺如何修正‘无永恒暴胀’条件。

Method: 采用两种独立方法：一是考察曲率扰动平移下正规序再加热面体积算符的行为；二是通过粗粒化分析获取其标度维数。

Result: 两种方法一致表明：当ε<0（与观测一致）时，再加热面体积的分形结构在宏观尺度上崩溃，导致其体积可重整化，从而弱化了永恒暴胀被排除的条件。

Conclusion: 尺度不变性的微小破缺（ε<0）足以破坏再加热面体积的分形性质，使永恒暴胀在重整化意义上变得可能，为暴胀宇宙学提供了新的理论容许空间。

Abstract: Recently, Cotler, Ivo, and Maldacena have shown that under scale invariance, eternal inflation is interpreted as a failure of the reheating surface volume renormalization due to an ill-defined scaling dimension. Meanwhile, observations suggest that scale invariance is slightly broken, which is parametrized by deviation of the spectral index from unity, $\varepsilon=n_s-1$. Motivated by this, we study how the scale invariance breaking effect modifies the no-eternal inflation condition as the renormalizability of the reheating surface volume. For this purpose, we adopt two independent approaches: investigating the behavior of the normal ordered reheating surface volume operator under a shift of the curvature perturbation, and analyzing the coarse-graining structure to obtain the scaling dimension. Both approaches commonly reveal that as long as $\varepsilon$ is negative, which is consistent with observations, the fractal structure of the reheating surface volume breaks down at macroscopic scale, moderating the no-eternal inflation condition.

</details>


### [143] [On connection between associators and 6j-symbols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08044)
*Elena Lanina,Artem Novokhatnii,Alexey Sleptsov,Radomir Stepanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新公式，将Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov方程导出的有理化Drinfeld结合子与代数U_q(g)的量子6j符号联系起来，并在多种李代数和表示情形下验证了该公式的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Drinfeld结合子与量子6j符号之间的明确关系，以深化对量子群表示理论及拓扑不变量的理解。

Method: 通过构造并验证特定李代数（如sl_2、sl_N、so_5）及其表示下的显式公式，结合解析计算与微扰展开方法进行证明与检验。

Result: 成功证明了U_q = U · Φ^KZ_Q这一公式在sl_2的若干表示情形下成立，并在sl_N和so_5的多个非平凡情形（含重数）中完成微扰验证。

Conclusion: 该公式为Drinfeld结合子与量子6j符号之间提供了统一的联系框架，拓展了其在量子拓扑与可积系统中的应用基础。

Abstract: We propose a new formula relating the rationalized Drinfeld associators obtained from the Knizhnik--Zamolodchikov equation to the quantum $6j$-symbols of the algebra $\mathcal{U}_q(\mathfrak{g})$: $ U_{q} = U \cdot Φ^{KZ}_{\mathbb{Q}} $. We prove this relation in the case of the $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ Lie algebra for the tensor cube of the fundamental and the first symmetric representation. We also check it perturbatively for the $\mathfrak{sl}_N$ algebra for representations $[2]\otimes [1] \otimes [1]$, $[2]\otimes [2] \otimes [1]$, the non-trivial cases with multiplicities --- $[2,1]^{\otimes 3}$, $[2,2]^{\otimes 3}$, and in the case of the $\mathfrak{so}_5$ Lie algebra for the tensor cube of the fundamental representation.

</details>


### [144] [Conformal Anomalies and Generalised Energy Conservation in De Sitter Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08113)
*Zong-Zhe Du*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了de Sitter时空散射振幅的广义能量守恒（GEC）条件与共形边界反常之间的对应关系，并通过GEC与全息自举法发现了新型四点相互作用，表明GEC解是全息一致解中的测度为零子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立de Sitter散射振幅的广义能量守恒条件与边界共形反常（含β函数贡献）之间的精确对应，并比较其与AdS/CFT中全息一致性的差异。

Method: 运用广义能量守恒（GEC）条件和全息自举法，分析偶数维扩展Poincaré时空中dS散射振幅，并研究含三个相同共形耦合标量与一个离散级数标量的四点接触相互作用。

Result: 发现GEC条件等价于无边界共形反常（含β函数），而AdS/CFT仅要求反常局域性；构造出新型四点相互作用；GEC解是全息一致解的测度零子集；结果与CFT关联函数重整化条件完全一致。

Conclusion: GEC提供了比全息一致性更强的约束，可用于检验尺度分离的非超对称AdS₄真空的全息一致性。

Abstract: We show that for bulk discrete series fields whose dual operators have integer conformal dimensions, the Generalised Energy Conservation (GEC) condition for de Sitter (dS) scattering amplitudes defined in even-dimensional extended Poincaré spacetime corresponds precisely to the absence of boundary conformal anomalies, including beta-function contributions, at the level of tree-level contact interactions. In contrast, holographic consistency in AdS/CFT enforces only the locality of the conformal anomaly.
  We find novel, unique four-point interactions involving three identical conformally coupled scalars and one discrete series scalar by performing both GEC and holographic bootstraps. This demonstrates that the solutions to GEC form a measure-zero subset of the holographically consistent solutions. Our holographic analysis agrees precisely with the expected renormalisation conditions for conformal field theory correlators. In addition, the constraint for a particular four-point configuration may be relevant for testing holographic consistency of scale-separated non-supersymmetric AdS$_4$ vacua.

</details>


### [145] [What a knot sees at a root of unity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08242)
*Ya. Kononov,A. Morozov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了HOMFLY-PT多项式在q为单位根时的普适因子分解结构，即其可分解为m-core部分与一个与m-ribbon相关的统一因子（即特殊多项式在A^m处的取值）的乘积，并利用Murnaghan-Nakayama规则和Adams运算给出证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释长期存在的HOMFLY-PT多项式在单位根处因子分解现象背后的隐藏结构与普适性原因。

Method: 运用Murnaghan-Nakayama规则和Adams运算，结合Chern-Simons理论中Wilson平均的解析延拓及缆结（cabling）技术，分析q为m次单位根时HOMFLY-PT多项式的代数结构。

Result: 证明当q²为m次本原单位根时，HOMFLY-PT多项式H_R对任意表示R普适地分解为m-core对应的多项式与同一因子H_{[m]}（即特殊多项式在A^m处的取值）的乘积；该因子源于投影算子在单位根处的奇异性所导出的有限braid和X_m。

Conclusion: HOMFLY-PT多项式在单位根处的因子分解并非偶然，而是由表示论中的深层对称性（如Adams运算）与拓扑量子场论结构共同决定的普适现象，从而解决了长期存在的因子分解之谜。

Abstract: HOMFLY-PT polynomials describe analytic continuation of Wilson averages in Chern-Simons theory to arbitrary complex values of two independent parameters $q$ and $A$. They also depend on the knot and representation of the gauge group. However, when $q^2$ is a primitive $m$-th root of unity, e.g. $q=\pm e^{iπ/m}$, these polynomials exhibit a universal (knot-independent) factorization: $H_R$ becomes the product of the polynomials for the $m$-core of the Young diagram $R$ and of one and the same factor $H_{[m]}$ for every $m$-ribbon of $R$. In all the examples which we computed this factor is just the special polynomial at $A^m$. At $q=\pm1$ factorization follows from the singularity of the cabled HOMFLY, which is the same for all representations. At $m>1$ the relevant singularity is in the projector, and what survives of it is a finite sum of braids $X_m$ -- the $m$-th power sum inside the cable, which can be freely moved through the other strands. For the proof we use the Murnaghan-Nakayama rule and the Adams operation. Discovery of this hidden structure explains the old factorization puzzle.

</details>


### [146] [Segre determinants and MHV gravity amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08252)
*Joris Koefler,Emanuele Ventura,Hongmiao Yu*

Main category: hep-th

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解树图MHV引力振幅的代数结构，特别是Hodges公式中分子的插值条件，寻求其多项式解的系统构造方法。

Method: 采用Segre-Veronese几何构造，利用Segre行列式生成满足插值条件的多项式解，并分析其线性独立性。

Result: 五点、六点Hodges分子可由Segre行列式给出；对n≥7，构造出与Hodges分子线性无关的新解；验证了n=7,8时此类独立解的存在性。

Conclusion: Segre-Veronese构造为MHV引力振幅分子提供了统一的多项式解框架，并揭示了n≥7时解空间维度大于1的新结构特征。

Abstract: We describe a Segre-Veronese construction of polynomial solutions to the interpolation conditions of the numerators of tree-level MHV gravity amplitudes given by Hodges' formula. The five- and six-point Hodges numerators arise from Segre determinants, while for every $n\geq7$ suitable Segre determinants lead to solutions linearly independent of the Hodges numerator. The existence of such independent elements for $n=7,8$ was recently established by Mai and Zhang.

</details>


### [147] [Relative-Entropy Flow Across the Page Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08397)
*Minghui Yu,Xian-Hui Ge*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞蒸发过程中不同半经典描述（如霍金辐射与复制品虫洞）之间的信息论差异及其演化，尤其是Page转变的信息本质。

Method: 基于复制技巧构建量子相对熵的计算框架，并将其应用于Jackiw-Teitelboim黑洞模型，比较霍金鞍点态与复制品虫洞鞍点态相对于精确辐射态的相对熵演化。

Result: 霍金鞍点的相对熵在蒸发中单调增加，而复制品虫洞鞍点的相对熵单调减少；二者之差在Page时间精确变号，标志着主导半经典描述的切换。

Conclusion: Page转变可被重新理解为不同粗粒化半经典描述之间相对熵代价的流动，揭示了黑洞蒸发中有效不可逆性背后的信息论结构。

Abstract: We develop a computational framework for the quantum relative entropy based on the replica trick and apply it to analyze the evaporation process method of Jackiw-Teitelboim black holes. We show that the relative entropies between the exact radiation state and the Hawking saddle state and the replica wormholes saddle state quantify information-theoretic costs inherent to coarse-grained semiclassical descriptions. During evaporation, the relative entropy for the Hawking saddle increases monotonically, reflecting a progressively higher thermodynamic cost of sustaining the Hawking description. Conversely, the relative entropy associated with the replica wormholes saddle decreases, which indicates that the description of connected geometries becomes progressively more accurate. Moreover, the sign of the difference between the two quantities reverses precisely at the Page time, providing an information-theoretic diagnosis for the transition between competing gravitational saddles. Our results recast the Page transition as a flow of relative entropy costs between competing semi-classical descriptions, revealing an information-theoretic structure underlying the emergence of effective irreversibility in coarse-grained black hole evaporation.

</details>


### [148] [Gauge-covariant Hadamard descent in Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08515)
*Lucio Vacchiano,Goffredo Chirco,Saverio Pascazio,Patrizia Vitale*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非阿贝尔杨-米尔斯理论从五维到四维的维度约化，提出Hadamard下降方法，并指出约化后理论中的标量场应为协变组合\widetildeΦ=ε-ι_YA而非简单取A_u；约化结果等价于一个伴随表示下的杨-米尔斯-希格斯理论；以U(2)为例分析了希格斯机制与对称性破缺；引入费米子后发现额外维联络分量可诱导赝标量型汤川耦合，经手征重定义可转化为费米子质量项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维规范理论如何通过维度约化自然导出四维物理模型（如希格斯机制、费米子质量生成），并澄清传统约化中对标量场身份的常见误解。

Method: 基于协变Cartan恒等式构建Hadamard下降框架，将维度约化表述为对沿约化方向平移不变的规范等价类的限制；系统推导约化后的拉氏量结构，并具体分析U(2)规范群下的希格斯势与对称破缺；引入费米子并考察其在u-独立规范下的耦合形式及手征重定义效果。

Result: 1）确认约化标量场是协变组合\widetildeΦ=ε-ι_YA，而非A_u；2）约化理论严格等价于四维杨-米尔斯-希格斯理论；3）U(2)情形下获得明确的对称性破缺模式SU(2)×U(1)→U(1)；4）额外维联络分量在u-独立规范下诱导赝标量汤川耦合，经手征变换可生成狄拉克质量项。

Conclusion: 维度约化不仅是几何操作，更需协变处理以保持物理完整性；Hadamard下降提供了一种更本质的约化视角，能自然统一希格斯场起源、对称性破缺与费米子质量生成机制。

Abstract: We study the dimensional reduction of non-Abelian Yang-Mills theory from five to four dimensions. In this framework, Hadamard descent is formulated as a restriction to gauge-equivalence classes that are invariant, up to gauge transformations, under translations along the reduced direction. The central result is that, prior to gauge fixing, the adjoint scalar of the reduced theory is not the connection component $A_u$ itself, but the gauge-covariant combination $\widetildeΦ=ε-ι_YA$, which follows naturally from the covariant Cartan identity. The usual identification with $A_u$ is recovered, up to the sign convention adopted here, in the $u$-independent gauge, while global holonomy data remain additional information to be specified separately. We show that the reduced theory takes the form of a Yang--Mills--Higgs theory with the Higgs field in the adjoint representation. As an example, we focus on the $U(2)$ gauge theory and analyze the Higgs mechanism, together with the associated symmetry breaking pattern, when the reduced theory is expanded around the ground state of the parent theory. We then include fermions and show that, in the $u$-independent gauge, the extra-dimensional component of the gauge connection generates a pseudoscalar Yukawa coupling which, on the vacuum configuration, can be converted into a fermion mass term by a chiral field redefinition, while the gauge interaction remains vectorial.

</details>


### [149] [Projected Yang-Mills Theory and the MacDowell-Mansouri Analogy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08566)
*Pedro D. Alvarez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有退化曲率动能算符的SO(2n)规范理论，通过正交复结构定义，其动能像为su(n)，伴随真空稳定子为U(n)；重点分析了投影算符固定、无约束伴随Higgs场及严格限制在SO(2n)/U(n)轨道上的Higgs场三种情形；在约束情形下，证明了存在一个规则弱曲率分支，其物理自由度数为2(n²−1)，局部等价于SU(n)杨–米尔斯理论；指出无约束情形中Higgs扰动可能导致动能秩变化，故自由度计数不普适；还讨论了代数分支，并将Stelle–West Higgs–gravity作用量与投影Pontryagin理论及满秩协变补全统一于Clifford对合框架下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解具有退化动能算符的规范理论的动力学结构及其与标准杨–米尔斯理论的关系，特别是探索Higgs机制如何影响规范场自由度计数与理论等价性。

Method: 采用非线性Dirac分析方法研究弱曲率分支；区分不同Higgs场约束条件（固定投影、无约束、轨道约束）；利用Clifford对合统一表述Stelle–West型引力耦合与投影Pontryagin理论；分析伴随表示真空结构与稳定子群。

Result: 在Higgs场严格限制于SO(2n)/U(n)轨道且动能系数为正时，得到含2(n²−1)个物理自由度的规则弱曲率分支，局部等价于SU(n) Yang–Mills理论；指出无约束情形下Higgs扰动可改变动能秩，导致自由度计数失效；刻画了可能的代数分支；构建了基于Clifford对合的统一框架，涵盖Stelle–West作用量、投影Pontryagin理论与满秩协变补全。

Conclusion: 退化动能规范理论在适当Higgs约束下可还原为标准杨–米尔斯理论，但其动力学敏感依赖于Higgs场的代数约束；Clifford对合提供了一种统一描述广义规范-引力耦合与拓扑项的有效几何语言。

Abstract: We study $SO(2n)$ gauge theories with a degenerate curvature kinetic operator defined by an orthogonal complex structure. The kinetic image is $\su(n)$, while the adjoint vacuum has stabilizer $U(n)$. We distinguish fixed projectors, unrestricted adjoint Higgs fields, and Higgs fields constrained exactly to the orbit $SO(2n)/U(n)$. For the constrained theory with a positive Higgs kinetic coefficient, the coset connection is auxiliary and the central connection is null. A nonlinear Dirac analysis establishes a regular weak-curvature branch with $2(n^2-1)$ physical degrees of freedom in four dimensions, locally equivalent to $SU(n)$ Yang--Mills theory. Normal Higgs displacements in the unrestricted theory can change the kinetic rank, so this count does not extend to that field space. We also characterize possible algebraic branches. We express the Stelle--West Higgs--gravity action through a common Clifford involution, and introduce projected Pontryagin theories and a full-rank equivariant completion.

</details>


### [150] [Connected Geometries in Gravitational Path Integrals, and Normalized No-Boundary Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08596)
*Jean-Luc Lehners*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出通过限制路径积分仅对连通且允许的几何结构求和，来缓解引力波函数归一化中的一些疑难问题。该方法在经典跃迁中给出合理结果，并提供非平凡的无边界概率，符合以往预期。同时强调了边界条件和积分路径对这些问题的额外影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决引力波函数归一化中出现的一些令人困惑的特征问题。

Method: 将路径积分限制在连通且允许的几何结构上，并分析边界条件与积分轮廓的影响。

Result: 该方法在经典跃迁中给出合理结果，并导出符合旧有预期的非平凡无边界概率。

Conclusion: 限制路径积分为连通、允许几何结构可有效缓解引力波函数归一化的疑难，并凸显边界条件与积分轮廓的关键作用。

Abstract: We argue that some of the puzzling features associated with the normalization of gravitational wave functions can be substantially alleviated by restricting the path integral to a sum over connected, allowable geometries. This prescription gives sensible results when applied to classical transitions and it provides non-trivial no-boundary probabilities, in line with old expectations. We highlight the additional impact of both boundary conditions and integration contours on these issues.

</details>


### [151] [Light bending at two-loop order from scattering amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08632)
*Jing Yang,Fa Peng Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用散射振幅方法，在第三阶后闵可夫斯基近似下计算了光子被无自旋大质量源（如黑洞）的保守偏转角，结果与史瓦西时空零测地线的弱偏转展开一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非线性经典光弯曲如何编码在平直时空背景下的圈图振幅中，并验证高阶后闵可夫斯基近似下广义相对论预言的光子偏转。

Method: 基于散射振幅方法，在重源和长程极限下采用几何光学近似；通过壳上递归构造五点光子-引力子树图振幅，并用五重割线连接三个标量-引力子顶点；利用多项式除法分解割线信息为十六项贡献；对源能量积分并保留近壳光子区域的空间积分；在静态软动量区域内将光子极点处方与三次树图迭代减法关联。

Result: 得到两圈阶的经典对数项，其横向傅里叶变换给出相互作用径向作用量，进而导出三阶偏转角。

Conclusion: 该结果与史瓦西度规下零测地线的弱偏转展开完全一致，证实了非线性经典光弯曲可通过平直时空中的量子场论圈图振幅精确描述。

Abstract: We calculate the conservative deflection of a photon by a massive spinless source, such as a black hole, at third post-Minkowskian order using scattering amplitudes. We work in the heavy-source and long-range limits and adopt the geometric-optics approximation. The required five-point photon-graviton tree amplitude is constructed by on-shell recursion and sewn to three scalar-graviton vertices across a five-fold cut. Polynomial division organizes the cut information into sixteen contributions, including the residues with fewer propagators. We perform the source energy integrations and retain the near-on-shell photon regions in the remaining spatial integrals. Within the static region in which all exchanged momenta are soft, we relate a photon pole prescription to the subtraction of the cubic tree iteration from the conservative kernel. The sum of the sixteen contributions determines the classical logarithmic term at two loops. Its transverse Fourier transform gives the interaction radial action and yields the third-order deflection angle. The result agrees with the weak-deflection expansion of Schwarzschild null geodesics and demonstrates how nonlinear classical light bending is encoded in loop amplitudes around flat spacetime.

</details>


### [152] [QCD_2: 't Hooft equation vs. relativistic Schroedinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08673)
*Andrei Smilga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维QCD在't Hooft极限下的介子谱，比较了积分Bethe-Salpeter方程与相对论性Schrödinger方程两种方法计算介子质量的结果，发现其渐近行为一致，但预渐近WKB修正存在差异，尤其对基态显著，而激发态差异迅速减小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维QCD中介子谱的精确计算方法及其不同理论框架（Bethe-Salpeter方程 vs. 相对论Schrödinger方程）之间的等价性与偏差。

Method: 采用积分Bethe-Salpeter方程和相对论性Schrödinger方程（含线性势）两种方法计算介子质量，并在半经典（WKB）框架下分析预渐近修正。

Result: 两种方法在主导半经典近似下结果一致；但预渐近WKB修正不同：基态差异显著，n=3时相对误差≤2.5%，且随n增大快速衰减。

Conclusion: 尽管两种方法在渐近极限下等价，预渐近修正揭示了其物理内涵的细微差别，提示在高精度谱学中需谨慎选择有效理论框架。

Abstract: We discuss 2-dimensional $QCD$ with a fundamental quark of mass $m$ in the 't Hooft limit ($N \to \infty, \, g^2 \to 0, \, g^2N =$ const). The spectrum of this theory includes a chain of meson states. The masses of these states can be evaluated by solving the proper integral Bethe-Salpeter equation. An alternative simple procedure consists in solving the Schrödinger equation for the relativistic Hamiltonian $$ \hat H\ =\ 2\sqrt{\hat p^2 + m^2} + σ|x| $$ with the string tension $σ= g^2N/4$. We compare the results obtained by these two methods. In the leading semiclassical approximation, they coincide.
  We show, however, that the preasymptotic WKB corrections to the masses calculated in these two ways are different. For the ground state, they play an essential role. But already for $n=3$, the relative difference between the two predictions does not exceed 2.5\% and rapidly falls off as $n$ increases.

</details>
